Itle:tay Omeoray anday Ulietjay

***ay ARTsTay OFay EtHay OJECTpRay UTENBERGgay EBOOKay OMEOray ANDay ULIETjay ***ay




EtHay AGEDYtRay OFay OMEOray ANDay ULIETjay

byay Illiamway Akespeareshay




Ontentscay

EtHay OLOGUEpRay.

ACTay Iay
Enescay Iay. Aay ublicpay aceplay.
Enescay IIay. Aay Eetstray.
Enescay IIIay. Oomray inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.
Enescay IVay. Aay Eetstray.
Enescay Vay. Aay Allhay inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.

ACTay IIay
ORUScHay.
Enescay Iay. Anay openay aceplay adjoiningay Apulet’scay Ardengay.
Enescay IIay. Apulet’scay Ardengay.
Enescay IIIay. Iarfray Awrence’slay Ellcay.
Enescay IVay. Aay Eetstray.
Enescay Vay. Apulet’scay Ardengay.
Enescay Ivay. Iarfray Awrence’slay Ellcay.

ACTay IIIay
Enescay Iay. Aay ublicpay Aceplay.
Enescay IIay. Aay Oomray inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.
Enescay IIIay. Iarfray Awrence’slay ellcay.
Enescay IVay. Aay Oomray inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.
Enescay Vay. Anay openay Allerygay otay Uliet’sjay Amberchay, overlookingay ethay Ardengay.

ACTay IVay
Enescay Iay. Iarfray Awrence’slay Ellcay.
Enescay IIay. Allhay inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.
Enescay IIIay. Uliet’sjay Amberchay.
Enescay IVay. Allhay inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.
Enescay Vay. Uliet’sjay Amber;chay Ulietjay onay ethay edbay.

ACTay Vay
Enescay Iay. Antuamay. Aay Eetstray.
Enescay IIay. Iarfray Awrence’slay Ellcay.
Enescay IIIay. Aay urchyard;chay inay itay aay Onumentmay elongingbay otay ethay Apuletscay.




Amatisdray Ersonæpay

ESCALUSay, Incepray ofay Eronavay.
ERCUTIOmay, insmankay otay ethay Incepray, anday iendfray otay Omeoray.
ARISpay, aay oungyay Oblemannay, insmankay otay ethay Incepray.
Agepay otay Arispay.

ONTAGUEmay, eadhay ofay aay Eronesevay amilyfay atay eudfay ithway ethay Apuletscay.
ADYlay ONTAGUEmay, ifeway otay Ontaguemay.
OMEOray, onsay otay Ontaguemay.
ENVOLIObay, ephewnay otay Ontaguemay, anday iendfray otay Omeoray.
ABRAMay, ervantsay otay Ontaguemay.
ALTHASARbay, ervantsay otay Omeoray.

APULETcay, eadhay ofay aay Eronesevay amilyfay atay eudfay ithway ethay Ontaguesmay.
ADYlay APULETcay, ifeway otay Apuletcay.
ULIETjay, aughterday otay Apuletcay.
ALTtYBay, ephewnay otay Adylay Apuletcay.
APULET’Scay OUSINcay, anay olday anmay.
URSEnay otay Ulietjay.
ETERpay, ervantsay otay Uliet’sjay Ursenay.
AMPSONsay, ervantsay otay Apuletcay.
EGORYgRay, ervantsay otay Apuletcay.
Ervantssay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay, aay Anciscanfray.
IARfRay OHNjay, ofay ethay amesay Orderay.
Anay Apothecaryay.
ORUScHay.
Eethray Usiciansmay.
Anay Officeray.
Itizenscay ofay Erona;vay everalsay Enmay anday Omenway, elationsray otay othbay ouses;hay
Askersmay, Uardsgay, Atchmenway anday Attendantsay.

ENEsCay. Uringday ethay eatergray artpay ofay ethay Ayplay inay Erona;vay onceay, inay ethay
Ifthfay Actay, atay Antuamay.




EtHay OLOGUEpRay


Enteray Oruschay.

ORUScHay.
Otway ouseholdshay, othbay alikeay inay ignityday,
Inay airfay Eronavay, erewhay eway aylay ouray enescay,
Omfray ancientay udgegray eakbray otay ewnay utinymay,
Erewhay ivilcay oodblay akesmay ivilcay andshay uncleanay.
Omfray orthfay ethay atalfay oinslay ofay esethay otway oesfay
Aay airpay ofay ar-cross’dstay overslay aketay eirthay ife;lay
Osewhay isadventur’dmay iteouspay overthrowsay
Othday ithway eirthay eathday urybay eirthay arents’pay ifestray.
Ethay earfulfay assagepay ofay eirthay eath-mark’dday ovelay,
Anday ethay ontinuancecay ofay eirthay arents’pay ageray,
Ichwhay, utbay eirthay ildren’schay enday, oughtnay ouldcay emoveray,
Isay ownay ethay otway ours’hay affictray ofay ouray age;stay
Ethay ichwhay, ifay ouyay ithway atientpay earsay attenday,
Atwhay erehay allshay issmay, ouray oiltay allshay ivestray otay endmay.

Exit_][_ay.




ACTay Iay

ENEsCay Iay. Aay ublicpay aceplay.


Enteray Ampsonsay anday Egorygray armeday ithway ordssway anday ucklersbay.

AMPSONsay.
Egorygray, onay myay ordway, e’llway otnay arrycay oalscay.

EGORYgRay.
Onay, orfay enthay eway ouldshay ebay ollierscay.

AMPSONsay.
Iay eanmay, ifay eway ebay inay olerchay, e’llway awdray.

EGORYgRay.
Ayay, ilewhay ouyay ivelay, awdray ouryay ecknay outay o’ay ethay ollarcay.

AMPSONsay.
Iay ikestray uicklyqay, eingbay ovedmay.

EGORYgRay.
Utbay outhay artay otnay uicklyqay ovedmay otay ikestray.

AMPSONsay.
Aay ogday ofay ethay ousehay ofay Ontaguemay ovesmay emay.

EGORYgRay.
Otay ovemay isay otay ir;stay anday otay ebay aliantvay isay otay and:stay ereforethay, ifay outhay
artay ovedmay, outhay unn’stray awayay.

AMPSONsay.
Aay ogday ofay atthay ousehay allshay ovemay emay otay andstay.
Iay illway aketay ethay allway ofay anyay anmay oray aidmay ofay Ontague’smay.

EGORYgRay.
Atthay owsshay eethay aay eakway aveslay, orfay ethay eakestway oesgay otay ethay allway.

AMPSONsay.
Uetray, anday ereforethay omenway, eingbay ethay eakerway esselsvay, areay everay ustthray otay
ethay all:way ereforethay Iay illway ushpay Ontague’smay enmay omfray ethay allway, anday
ustthray ishay aidsmay otay ethay allway.

EGORYgRay.
Ethay uarrelqay isay etweenbay ouray astersmay anday usay eirthay enmay.

AMPSONsay.
is’Tay allay oneay, Iay illway owshay elfmysay aay ant:tyray enwhay Iay avehay oughtfay ithway ethay
enmay Iay illway ebay ivilcay ithway ethay aidsmay, Iay illway utcay offay eirthay eadshay.

EGORYgRay.
Ethay eadshay ofay ethay aids?may

AMPSONsay.
Ayay, ethay eadshay ofay ethay aidsmay, oray eirthay aidenheads;may aketay itay inay atwhay ensesay
outhay iltway.

EGORYgRay.
Eythay ustmay aketay itay inay ensesay atthay eelfay itay.

AMPSONsay.
Emay eythay allshay eelfay ilewhay Iay amay ableay otay and:stay anday is’tay ownknay Iay amay aay
ettypray iecepay ofay eshflay.

EGORYgRay.
is’Tay ellway outhay artay otnay ish;fay ifay outhay adsthay, outhay adsthay eenbay oorpay Ohnjay.
Awdray thyay ool;tay erehay omescay ofay ethay ousehay ofay Ontaguesmay.

Enteray Abramay anday Althasarbay.

AMPSONsay.
Myay akednay eaponway isay out:ay uarrelqay, Iay illway ackbay eethay.

EGORYgRay.
Ow?hay Urntay thyay ackbay anday un?ray

AMPSONsay.
Earfay emay otnay.

EGORYgRay.
Onay, arry;may Iay earfay ee!thay

AMPSONsay.
Etlay usay aketay ethay awlay ofay ouray ides;say etlay emthay eginbay.

EGORYgRay.
Iay illway ownfray asay Iay asspay byay, anday etlay emthay aketay itay asay eythay istlay.

AMPSONsay.
Aynay, asay eythay areday. Iay illway itebay myay umbthay atay emthay, ichwhay isay isgraceday otay
emthay ifay eythay earbay itay.

ABRAMay.
Oday ouyay itebay ouryay umbthay atay usay, ir?say

AMPSONsay.
Iay oday itebay myay umbthay, irsay.

ABRAMay.
Oday ouyay itebay ouryay umbthay atay usay, ir?say

AMPSONsay.
Isay ethay awlay ofay ouray idesay ifay Iay aysay ay?ay

EGORYgRay.
Onay.

AMPSONsay.
Onay irsay, Iay oday otnay itebay myay umbthay atay ouyay, ir;say utbay Iay itebay myay umbthay, irsay.

EGORYgRay.
Oday ouyay uarrelqay, ir?say

ABRAMay.
Uarrelqay, ir?say Onay, irsay.

AMPSONsay.
Utbay ifay ouyay oday, irsay, Iay amay orfay ouyay. Iay ervesay asay oodgay aay anmay asay ouyay.

ABRAMay.
Onay etterbay.

AMPSONsay.
Ellway, irsay.

Enteray Envoliobay.

EGORYgRay.
Aysay etter;bay erehay omescay oneay ofay myay aster’smay insmenkay.

AMPSONsay.
Esyay, etterbay, irsay.

ABRAMay.
Ouyay ielay.

AMPSONsay.
Awdray, ifay ouyay ebay enmay. Egorygray, ememberray thyay ashingway owblay.

ey[_Thay ight_]fay.

ENVOLIObay.
Artpay, ools!fay utpay upay ouryay ordssway, ouyay owknay otnay atwhay ouyay oday.

eats[_Bay ownday eirthay ords_]sway.

Enteray Alttybay.

ALTtYBay.
Atwhay, artay outhay awndray amongay esethay eartlesshay inds?hay
Urntay eethay Envoliobay, ooklay uponay thyay eathday.

ENVOLIObay.
Iay oday utbay eepkay ethay eacepay, utpay upay thyay ordsway,
Oray anagemay itay otay artpay esethay enmay ithway emay.

ALTtYBay.
Atwhay, awndray, anday alktay ofay eace?pay Iay atehay ethay ordway
Asay Iay atehay ellhay, allay Ontaguesmay, anday ee:thay
Avehay atay eethay, owardcay.

ey[_Thay ight_]fay.

Enteray eethray oray ourfay Itizenscay ithway ubsclay.

IRSTfay ITIZENcay.
Ubsclay, illsbay anday artisans!pay Ike!stray Eatbay emthay own!day
Ownday ithway ethay Apulets!cay Ownday ithway ethay Ontagues!may

Enteray Apuletcay inay ishay owngay, anday Adylay Apuletcay.

APULETcay.
Atwhay oisenay isay is?thay Ivegay emay myay onglay ordsway, o!hay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Aay utchcray, aay utch!cray Whyay allcay ouyay orfay aay ord?sway

APULETcay.
Myay ordsway, Iay ay!say Olday Ontaguemay isay omecay,
Anday ourishesflay ishay adeblay inay itespay ofay emay.

Enteray Ontaguemay anday ishay Adylay Ontaguemay.

ONTAGUEmay.
Outhay illainvay Apulet!cay Oldhay emay otnay, etlay emay ogay.

ADYlay ONTAGUEmay.
Outhay altshay otnay irstay oneay ootfay otay eeksay aay oefay.

Enteray Incepray Escalusay, ithway Attendantsay.

INCEpRay.
Ebelliousray ubjectssay, enemiesay otay eacepay,
Ofanerspray ofay isthay eighbour-stainednay eel—stay,
Illway eythay otnay ear?hay Atwhay, o!hay Ouyay enmay, ouyay eastsbay,
Atthay uenchqay ethay irefay ofay ouryay erniciouspay ageray
Ithway urplepay ountainsfay issuingay omfray ouryay einsvay,
Onay ainpay ofay orturetay, omfray osethay oodyblay andshay
Owthray ouryay istemper’dmay eaponsway otay ethay oundgray
Anday earhay ethay entencesay ofay ouryay ovedmay incepray.
Eethray ivilcay awlsbray, edbray ofay anay airyay ordway,
Byay eethay, olday Apuletcay, anday Ontaguemay,
Avehay icethray isturb’dday ethay uietqay ofay ouray eetsstray,
Anday ademay Erona’svay ancientay itizenscay
Astcay byay eirthay avegray eseemingbay ornamentsay,
Otay ieldway olday artisanspay, inay andshay asay olday,
Anker’dcay ithway eacepay, otay artpay ouryay anker’dcay atehay.
Ifay everay ouyay isturbday ouray eetsstray againay,
Ouryay iveslay allshay aypay ethay orfeitfay ofay ethay eacepay.
Orfay isthay imetay allay ethay estray epartday away:ay
Ouyay, Apuletcay, allshay ogay alongay ithway emay,
Anday Ontaguemay, omecay ouyay isthay afternoonay,
Otay owknay ouray artherfay easureplay inay isthay asecay,
Otay olday Ee-townfray, ouray ommoncay udgement-placejay.
Onceay oremay, onay ainpay ofay eathday, allay enmay epartday.

Exeunt[_ay Incepray anday Attendants;ay Apuletcay, Adylay Apuletcay, Alttybay,
Itizenscay anday Ervants_]say.

ONTAGUEmay.
Owhay etsay isthay ancientay uarrelqay ewnay abroach?ay
Eakspay, ephewnay, ereway ouyay byay enwhay itay egan?bay

ENVOLIObay.
Erehay ereway ethay ervantssay ofay ouryay adversaryay
Anday oursyay, oseclay ightingfay ereay Iay idday approachay.
Iay ewdray otay artpay emthay, inay ethay instantay amecay
Ethay ieryfay Alttybay, ithway ishay ordsway epar’dpray,
Ichwhay, asay ehay eath’dbray efianceday otay myay earsay,
Ehay ungsway aboutay ishay eadhay, anday utcay ethay indsway,
Owhay othingnay urthay ithalway, iss’dhay imhay inay ornscay.
Ilewhay eway ereway interchangingay uststhray anday owsblay
Amecay oremay anday oremay, anday oughtfay onay artpay anday artpay,
Illtay ethay Incepray amecay, owhay artedpay eitheray artpay.

ADYlay ONTAGUEmay.
Oay erewhay isay Omeoray, awsay ouyay imhay oday?tay
Ightray adglay Iay amay ehay asway otnay atay isthay ayfray.

ENVOLIObay.
Adammay, anay ourhay eforebay ethay orshipp’dway unsay
Eer’dpay orthfay ethay oldengay indowway ofay ethay eastay,
Aay oubledtray indmay avedray emay otay alkway abroaday,
Erewhay underneathay ethay ovegray ofay amoresycay
Atthay estwardway ootethray omfray isthay itycay idesay,
Osay earlyay alkingway idday Iay eesay ouryay onsay.
Owardstay imhay Iay ademay, utbay ehay asway areway ofay emay,
Anday olestay intoay ethay overtcay ofay ethay oodway.
Iay, easuringmay ishay affectionsay byay myay ownay,
Ichwhay enthay ostmay oughtsay erewhay ostmay ightmay otnay ebay oundfay,
Eingbay oneay ootay anymay byay myay earyway elfsay,
Ursu’dpay myay umourhay, otnay ursuingpay ishay,
Anday adlyglay unn’dshay owhay adlyglay edflay omfray emay.

ONTAGUEmay.
Anymay aay orningmay athhay ehay erethay eenbay eensay,
Ithway earstay augmentingay ethay eshfray orning’smay ewday,
Addingay otay oudsclay oremay oudsclay ithway ishay eepday ighs;say
Utbay allay osay oonsay asay ethay all-cheeringay unsay
Ouldshay inay ethay arthestfay eastay eginbay otay awdray
Ethay adyshay urtainscay omfray Aurora’say edbay,
Awayay omfray ightlay ealsstay omehay myay eavyhay onsay,
Anday ivatepray inay ishay amberchay enspay imselfhay,
Utsshay upay ishay indowsway, ockslay airfay aylightday outay
Anday akesmay imselfhay anay artificialay ightnay.
Ackblay anday ortentouspay ustmay isthay umourhay ovepray,
Unlessay oodgay ounselcay aymay ethay ausecay emoveray.

ENVOLIObay.
Myay oblenay uncleay, oday ouyay owknay ethay ause?cay

ONTAGUEmay.
Iay eithernay owknay itay ornay ancay earnlay ofay imhay.

ENVOLIObay.
Avehay ouyay importun’day imhay byay anyay eans?may

ONTAGUEmay.
Othbay byay elfmysay anday anymay otheray iends;fray
Utbay ehay, ishay ownay affections’ay ounsellorcay,
Isay otay imself—Ihay illway otnay aysay owhay ue—tray
Utbay otay imselfhay osay ecretsay anday osay oseclay,
Osay arfay omfray oundingsay anday iscoveryday,
Asay isay ethay udbay itbay ithway anay enviousay ormway
Ereay ehay ancay eadspray ishay eetsway eaveslay otay ethay airay,
Oray edicateday ishay eautybay otay ethay unsay.
Ouldcay eway utbay earnlay omfray encewhay ishay orrowssay owgray,
Eway ouldway asay illinglyway ivegay urecay asay owknay.

Enteray Omeoray.

ENVOLIObay.
Eesay, erewhay ehay omescay. Osay easeplay ouyay epstay aside;ay
I’llay owknay ishay ievancegray oray ebay uchmay eniedday.

ONTAGUEmay.
Iay ouldway outhay ertway osay appyhay byay thyay aystay
Otay earhay uetray iftshray. Omecay, adammay, et’slay awayay,

Exeunt[_ay Ontaguemay anday Adylay Ontague_]may.

ENVOLIObay.
Oodgay orrowmay, ousincay.

OMEOray.
Isay ethay ayday osay oung?yay

ENVOLIObay.
Utbay ewnay uckstray inenay.

OMEOray.
Ayay emay, adsay ourshay eemsay onglay.
Asway atthay myay atherfay atthay entway encehay osay ast?fay

ENVOLIObay.
Itay asway. Atwhay adnesssay engthenslay Omeo’sray ours?hay

OMEOray.
Otnay avinghay atthay ichwhay, avinghay, akesmay emthay ortshay.

ENVOLIObay.
Inay ove?lay

OMEOray.
Outay.

ENVOLIObay.
Ofay ove?lay

OMEOray.
Outay ofay erhay avourfay erewhay Iay amay inay ovelay.

ENVOLIObay.
Alasay atthay ovelay osay entlegay inay ishay iewvay,
Ouldshay ebay osay annoustyray anday oughray inay oofpray.

OMEOray.
Alasay atthay ovelay, osewhay iewvay isay uffledmay illstay,
Ouldshay, ithoutway eyesay, eesay athwayspay otay ishay ill!way
Erewhay allshay eway ine?day Oay e!may Atwhay ayfray asway ere?hay
Etyay elltay emay otnay, orfay Iay avehay eardhay itay allay.
Ere’shay uchmay otay oday ithway atehay, utbay oremay ithway ove:lay
Whyay, enthay, Oay awlingbray ove!lay Oay ovinglay ate!hay
Oay anythingay, ofay othingnay irstfay eate!cray
Oay eavyhay ightness!lay erioussay anity!vay
Isshapenmay aoschay ofay ell-seemingway orms!fay
Eatherfay ofay eadlay, ightbray okesmay, oldcay irefay, icksay ealth!hay
Ill-wakingstay eepslay, atthay isay otnay atwhay itay is!ay
Isthay ovelay eelfay Iay, atthay eelfay onay ovelay inay isthay.
Ostday outhay otnay augh?lay

ENVOLIObay.
Onay ozcay, Iay atherray eepway.

OMEOray.
Oodgay earthay, atay at?whay

ENVOLIObay.
Atay thyay oodgay eart’shay oppressionay.

OMEOray.
Whyay uchsay isay ove’slay ansgressiontray.
Iefsgray ofay inemay ownay ielay eavyhay inay myay eastbray,
Ichwhay outhay iltway opagatepray otay avehay itay estpray
Ithway oremay ofay inethay. Isthay ovelay atthay outhay asthay ownshay
Othday adday oremay iefgray otay ootay uchmay ofay inemay ownay.
Ovelay isay aay okesmay ademay ithway ethay umefay ofay ighs;say
Eingbay urg’dpay, aay irefay arklingspay inay overs’lay eyes;ay
Eingbay ex’dvay, aay easay ourish’dnay ithway overs’lay ears:tay
Atwhay isay itay else?ay Aay adnessmay ostmay iscreetday,
Aay okingchay allgay, anday aay eservingpray eetsway.
Arewellfay, myay ozcay.

oing_][_Gay.

ENVOLIObay.
Oft!say Iay illway ogay along:ay
Anday ifay ouyay eavelay emay osay, ouyay oday emay ongwray.

OMEOray.
Ut!tay Iay avehay ostlay elf;mysay Iay amay otnay erehay.
Isthay isay otnay Omeoray, e’shay omesay otheray erewhay.

ENVOLIObay.
Elltay emay inay adnesssay owhay isay atthay ouyay ove?lay

OMEOray.
Atwhay, allshay Iay oangray anday elltay ee?thay

ENVOLIObay.
Oan!gray Whyay, o;nay utbay adlysay elltay emay owhay.

OMEOray.
Idbay aay icksay anmay inay adnesssay akemay ishay illway,
Aay ordway illay urg’day otay oneay atthay isay osay illay.
Inay adnesssay, ousincay, Iay oday ovelay aay omanway.

ENVOLIObay.
Iay aim’day osay earnay enwhay Iay uppos’dsay ouyay ov’dlay.

OMEOray.
Aay ightray oodgay arkmanmay, anday e’sshay airfay Iay ovelay.

ENVOLIObay.
Aay ightray airfay arkmay, airfay ozcay, isay oonestsay ithay.

OMEOray.
Ellway, inay atthay ithay ouyay iss:may e’llshay otnay ebay ithay
Ithway Upid’scay arroway, eshay athhay Ian’sday it;way
Anday inay ongstray oofpray ofay astitychay ellway arm’day,
Omfray ove’slay eakway ildishchay owbay eshay iveslay uncharm’day.
Eshay illway otnay aystay ethay iegesay ofay ovinglay ermstay
Ornay idebay encounterth’ay ofay assailingay eyesay,
Ornay opeay erhay aplay otay aint-seducingsay old:gay
Oay e’sshay ichray inay eautybay, onlyay oorpay
Atthay enwhay eshay iesday, ithway eautybay iesday erhay orestay.

ENVOLIObay.
Enthay eshay athhay ornsway atthay eshay illway illstay ivelay aste?chay

OMEOray.
Eshay athhay, anday inay atthay aringspay akesmay ugehay aste;way
Orfay eautybay arv’dstay ithway erhay everitysay,
Utscay eautybay offay omfray allay osteritypay.
Eshay isay ootay airfay, ootay ise;way iselyway ootay airfay,
Otay eritmay issblay byay akingmay emay espairday.
Eshay athhay orswornfay otay ovelay, anday inay atthay owvay
Oday Iay ivelay eadday, atthay ivelay otay elltay itay ownay.

ENVOLIObay.
Ebay ul’dray byay emay, orgetfay otay inkthay ofay erhay.

OMEOray.
Oay eachtay emay owhay Iay ouldshay orgetfay otay inkthay.

ENVOLIObay.
Byay ivinggay ibertylay untoay inethay eyes;ay
Examineay otheray eautiesbay.

OMEOray.
is’Tay ethay ayway
Otay allcay ershay, exquisiteay, inay uestionqay oremay.
Esethay appyhay asksmay atthay isskay airfay adies’lay owsbray,
Eingbay ackblay, utspay usay inay indmay eythay idehay ethay air;fay
Ehay atthay isay uckenstray indblay annotcay orgetfay
Ethay eciouspray easuretray ofay ishay eyesightay ostlay.
Owshay emay aay istressmay atthay isay assingpay airfay,
Atwhay othday erhay eautybay ervesay utbay asay aay otenay
Erewhay Iay aymay eadray owhay ass’dpay atthay assingpay air?fay
Arewellfay, outhay anstcay otnay eachtay emay otay orgetfay.

ENVOLIObay.
I’llay aypay atthay octrineday, oray elseay ieday inay ebtday.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IIay. Aay Eetstray.

Enteray Apuletcay, Arispay anday Ervantsay.

APULETcay.
Utbay Ontaguemay isay oundbay asay ellway asay Iay,
Inay enaltypay alike;ay anday is’tay otnay ardhay, Iay inkthay,
Orfay enmay osay olday asay eway otay eepkay ethay eacepay.

ARISpay.
Ofay onourablehay eckoningray areay ouyay othbay,
Anday itypay is’tay ouyay iv’dlay atay oddsay osay onglay.
Utbay ownay myay ordlay, atwhay aysay ouyay otay myay uit?say

APULETcay.
Utbay ayingsay o’eray atwhay Iay avehay aidsay eforebay.
Myay ildchay isay etyay aay angerstray inay ethay orldway,
Eshay athhay otnay eensay ethay angechay ofay ourteenfay ears;yay
Etlay otway oremay ummerssay itherway inay eirthay idepray
Ereay eway aymay inkthay erhay iperay otay ebay aay idebray.

ARISpay.
Oungeryay anthay eshay areay appyhay othersmay ademay.

APULETcay.
Anday ootay oonsay arr’dmay areay osethay osay earlyay ademay.
Ethay earthay athhay allowedsway allay myay opeshay utbay eshay,
Eshay isay ethay opefulhay adylay ofay myay earth:ay
Utbay ooway erhay, entlegay Arispay, etgay erhay earthay,
Myay illway otay erhay onsentcay isay utbay aay art;pay
Anday eshay agreeay, ithinway erhay opescay ofay oicechay
Ieslay myay onsentcay anday airfay accordingay oicevay.
Isthay ightnay Iay oldhay anay olday accustom’day eastfay,
Eretowhay Iay avehay inviteday anymay aay uestgay,
Uchsay asay Iay ovelay, anday ouyay amongay ethay orestay,
Oneay oremay, ostmay elcomeway, akesmay myay umbernay oremay.
Atay myay oorpay ousehay ooklay otay eholdbay isthay ightnay
Earth-treadingay arsstay atthay akemay arkday eavenhay ight:lay
Uchsay omfortcay asay oday ustylay oungyay enmay eelfay
Enwhay ellway apparell’day Aprilay onay ethay eelhay
Ofay impinglay interway eadstray, evenay uchsay elightday
Amongay eshfray emalefay udsbay allshay ouyay isthay ightnay
Inheritay atay myay ousehay. Earhay allay, allay eesay,
Anday ikelay erhay ostmay osewhay eritmay ostmay allshay e:bay
Ichwhay, onay oremay iewvay ofay anymay, inemay, eingbay oneay,
Aymay andstay inay umbernay, oughthay inay eckoningray onenay.
Omecay, ogay ithway emay. Ogay, irrahsay, udgetray aboutay
Oughthray airfay Erona;vay indfay osethay ersonspay outay
Osewhay amesnay areay ittenwray erethay, ives[_gay aay aper_]pay anday otay emthay aysay,
Myay ousehay anday elcomeway onay eirthay easureplay aystay.

Exeunt[_ay Apuletcay anday Aris_]pay.

ERVANTsay.
Indfay emthay outay osewhay amesnay areay ittenwray ere!hay Itay isay ittenwray atthay ethay
oemakershay ouldshay eddlemay ithway ishay ardyay anday ethay ailortay ithway ishay astlay, ethay
isherfay ithway ishay encilpay, anday ethay ainterpay ithway ishay ets;nay utbay Iay amay entsay otay
indfay osethay ersonspay osewhay amesnay areay erehay itwray, anday ancay evernay indfay atwhay
amesnay ethay itingwray ersonpay athhay erehay itwray. Iay ustmay otay ethay earnedlay. Inay oodgay
ime!tay

Enteray Envoliobay anday Omeoray.

ENVOLIObay.
Uttay, anmay, oneay irefay urnsbay outay another’say urningbay,
Oneay ainpay isay essen’dlay byay another’say anguish;ay
Urntay iddygay, anday ebay olphay byay ackwardbay urning;tay
Oneay esperateday iefgray urescay ithway another’say anguish:lay
Aketay outhay omesay ewnay infectionay otay thyay eyeay,
Anday ethay ankray oisonpay ofay ethay olday illway ieday.

OMEOray.
Ouryay antainplay eaflay isay excellentay orfay atthay.

ENVOLIObay.
Orfay atwhay, Iay aypray ee?thay

OMEOray.
Orfay ouryay okenbray inshay.

ENVOLIObay.
Whyay, Omeoray, artay outhay ad?may

OMEOray.
Otnay admay, utbay oundbay oremay anthay aay admanmay is:ay
Utshay upay inay isonpray, eptkay ithoutway myay oodfay,
Ipp’dwhay anday ormentedtay and—God-denay, oodgay ellowfay.

ERVANTsay.
Odgay i’gay o-dengay. Iay aypray, irsay, ancay ouyay ead?ray

OMEOray.
Ayay, inemay ownay ortunefay inay myay iserymay.

ERVANTsay.
Erhapspay ouyay avehay earnedlay itay ithoutway ookbay.
Utbay Iay aypray, ancay ouyay eadray anythingay ouyay ee?say

OMEOray.
Ayay, Ifay Iay owknay ethay etterslay anday ethay anguagelay.

ERVANTsay.
Eyay aysay onestlyhay, estray ouyay erry!may

OMEOray.
Aystay, ellow;fay Iay ancay eadray.

ENVOLIObay.
Atay isthay amesay ancientay eastfay ofay Apulet’scay
Upssay ethay airfay Osalineray omwhay outhay osay ov’st;lay
Ithway allay ethay admireday eautiesbay ofay Eronavay.
Ogay itherthay anday ithway unattainteday eyeay,
Omparecay erhay acefay ithway omesay atthay Iay allshay owshay,
Anday Iay illway akemay eethay inkthay thyay answay aay owcray.

OMEOray.
Enwhay ethay evoutday eligionray ofay inemay eyeay
Aintainsmay uchsay alsehoodfay, enthay urntay earstay otay ire;fay
Anday esethay owhay, oftenay own’ddray, ouldcay evernay ieday,
Ansparenttray ereticshay, ebay urntbay orfay iarslay.
Oneay airerfay anthay myay ove?lay Ethay all-seeingay unsay
E’ernay awsay erhay atchmay incesay irstfay ethay orldway egunbay.

ENVOLIObay.
Uttay, ouyay awsay erhay airfay, onenay elseay eingbay byay,
Erselfhay ois’dpay ithway erselfhay inay eitheray eye:ay
Utbay inay atthay alcrystay alesscay etlay erethay ebay eigh’dway
Ouryay ady’slay ovelay againstay omesay otheray aidmay
Atthay Iay illway owshay ouyay iningshay atay isthay eastfay,
Anday eshay allshay antscay owshay ellway atthay ownay owsshay estbay.

OMEOray.
I’llay ogay alongay, onay uchsay ightsay otay ebay ownshay,
Utbay otay ejoiceray inay endoursplay ofay myay ownay.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IIIay. Oomray inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.

Enteray Adylay Apuletcay anday Ursenay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ursenay, ere’swhay myay aughter?day Allcay erhay orthfay otay emay.

URSEnay.
Ownay, byay myay aidenheadmay, atay elvetway earyay olday,
Iay adebay erhay omecay. Atwhay, amb!lay Atwhay adybird!lay
Odgay orbid!fay Ere’swhay isthay irl?gay Atwhay, Uliet!jay

Enteray Ulietjay.

ULIETjay.
Owhay ownay, owhay alls?cay

URSEnay.
Ouryay othermay.

ULIETjay.
Adammay, Iay amay erehay. Atwhay isay ouryay ill?way

ADYlay APULETcay.
Isthay isay ethay attermay. Ursenay, ivegay eavelay awhileay,
Eway ustmay alktay inay ecretsay. Ursenay, omecay ackbay againay,
Iay avehay emember’dray emay, ou’sthay earhay ouray ounselcay.
Outhay owestknay myay aughter’sday ofay aay ettypray ageay.

URSEnay.
Aithfay, Iay ancay elltay erhay ageay untoay anay ourhay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
E’sshay otnay ourteenfay.

URSEnay.
I’llay aylay ourteenfay ofay myay eethtay,
Anday etyay, otay myay eentay ebay itay okenspay, Iay avehay utbay ourfay,
Eshay isay otnay ourteenfay. Owhay onglay isay itay ownay
Otay Ammas-tide?lay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Aay ortnightfay anday odday aysday.

URSEnay.
Evenay oray odday, ofay allay aysday inay ethay earyay,
Omecay Ammaslay Eveay atay ightnay allshay eshay ebay ourteenfay.
Usansay anday e—Godshay, estray allay Istianchray ouls!—say
Ereway ofay anay ageay. Ellway, Usansay isay ithway Od;gay
Eshay asway ootay oodgay orfay emay. Utbay asay Iay aidsay,
Onay Ammaslay Eveay atay ightnay allshay eshay ebay ourteen;fay
Atthay allshay eshay, arry;may Iay ememberray itay ellway.
is’Tay incesay ethay earthquakeay ownay elevenay ears;yay
Anday eshay asway ean’d—Iway, evernay allshay orgetfay it—ay,
Ofay allay ethay aysday ofay ethay earyay, uponay atthay ay:day
Orfay Iay adhay enthay aidlay ormwoodway otay myay ugday,
Ittingsay inay ethay unsay underay ethay ovehouseday all;way
Myay ordlay anday ouyay ereway enthay atay Antua:may
Aynay, Iay oday earbay aay ainbray. Utbay asay Iay aidsay,
Enwhay itay idday astetay ethay ormwoodway onay ethay ipplenay
Ofay myay ugday anday eltfay itay itterbay, ettypray oolfay,
Otay eesay itay etchytay, anday allfay outay ithway ethay ug!day
Akeshay, uothqay ethay ovehouse:day as’tway onay eednay, Iay owtray,
Otay idbay emay udgetray.
Anday incesay atthay imetay itay isay elevenay ears;yay
Orfay enthay eshay ouldcay andstay alone;ay aynay, byay oodth’ray
Eshay ouldcay avehay unray anday addledway allay about;ay
Orfay evenay ethay ayday eforebay eshay okebray erhay owbray,
Anday enthay myay usband—Godhay, ebay ithway ishay oul!say
Aay asway aay errymay an—tookmay, upay ethay ild:chay
ea’‘Yay, uothqay ehay, ost‘day outhay allfay uponay thyay ace?fay
Outhay iltway allfay ackwardbay enwhay outhay asthay oremay it;way
Iltway outhay otnay, Ule?’jay anday, byay myay olidamehay,
Ethay ettypray etchwray eftlay ingcryay, anday aidsay Ay’‘ay.
Otay eesay ownay owhay aay estjay allshay omecay aboutay.
Iay arrantway, anday Iay ouldshay ivelay aay ousandthay earsyay,
Iay evernay ouldshay orgetfay itay. ilt‘Way outhay otnay, Ule?’jay uothqay e;hay
Anday, ettypray oolfay, itay intedstay, anday aidsay Ay’‘ay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Enoughay ofay is;thay Iay aypray eethay oldhay thyay eacepay.

URSEnay.
Esyay, adammay, etyay Iay annotcay oosechay utbay aughlay,
Otay inkthay itay ouldshay eavelay ingcryay, anday aysay Ay’;‘ay
Anday etyay Iay arrantway itay adhay uponay itay owbray
Aay umpbay asay igbay asay aay oungyay ockerel’scay one;stay
Aay erilouspay ockknay, anday itay iedcray itterlybay.
ea’‘Yay, uothqay myay usbandhay, all’st‘fay uponay thyay ace?fay
Outhay iltway allfay ackwardbay enwhay outhay omestcay otay age;ay
Iltway outhay otnay, Ule?’jay itay intedstay, anday aidsay Ay’‘ay.

ULIETjay.
Anday intstay outhay ootay, Iay aypray eethay, Ursenay, aysay Iay.

URSEnay.
Eacepay, Iay avehay oneday. Odgay arkmay eethay otay ishay acegray
Outhay astway ethay ettiestpray abebay atthay e’eray Iay urs’d:nay
Anday Iay ightmay ivelay otay eesay eethay arriedmay onceay, Iay avehay myay ishway.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Arrymay, atthay arrymay isay ethay eryvay emethay
Iay amecay otay alktay ofay. Elltay emay, aughterday Ulietjay,
Owhay andsstay ouryay ispositionday otay ebay arried?may

ULIETjay.
Itay isay anay onourhay atthay Iay eamdray otnay ofay.

URSEnay.
Anay onour!hay Ereway otnay Iay inethay onlyay ursenay,
Iay ouldway aysay outhay adsthay uck’dsay isdomway omfray thyay eattay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ellway, inkthay ofay arriagemay ow:nay oungeryay anthay ouyay,
Erehay inay Eronavay, adieslay ofay esteemay,
Areay ademay alreadyay othersmay. Byay myay ountcay
Iay asway ouryay othermay uchmay uponay esethay earsyay
Atthay ouyay areay ownay aay aidmay. Usthay, enthay, inay ief;bray
Ethay aliantvay Arispay eekssay ouyay orfay ishay ovelay.

URSEnay.
Aay anmay, oungyay ady!lay Adylay, uchsay aay anmay
Asay allay ethay orld—whyway e’shay aay anmay ofay axway.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Erona’svay ummersay athhay otnay uchsay aay owerflay.

URSEnay.
Aynay, e’shay aay owerflay, inay aithfay aay eryvay owerflay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Atwhay aysay ouyay, ancay ouyay ovelay ethay entleman?gay
Isthay ightnay ouyay allshay eholdbay imhay atay ouray east;fay
Eadray o’eray ethay olumevay ofay oungyay Aris’pay acefay,
Anday indfay elightday itwray erethay ithway eauty’sbay enpay.
Examineay everyay arriedmay ineamentlay,
Anday eesay owhay oneay anotheray endslay ontent;cay
Anday atwhay obscur’day inay isthay airfay olumevay ieslay,
Indfay ittenwray inay ethay argentmay ofay ishay eyesay.
Isthay eciouspray ookbay ofay ovelay, isthay unbounday overlay,
Otay eautifybay imhay, onlyay ackslay aay over:cay
Ethay ishfay iveslay inay ethay ea;say anday is’tay uchmay idepray
Orfay airfay ithoutway ethay airfay ithinway otay idehay.
Atthay ookbay inay any’smay eyesay othday areshay ethay oryglay,
Atthay inay oldgay aspsclay ockslay inay ethay oldengay ory;stay
Osay allshay ouyay areshay allay atthay ehay othday ossesspay,
Byay avinghay imhay, akingmay ourselfyay onay esslay.

URSEnay.
Onay esslay, aynay iggerbay. Omenway owgray byay enmay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Eakspay ieflybray, ancay ouyay ikelay ofay Aris’pay ove?lay

ULIETjay.
I’llay ooklay otay ikelay, ifay ookinglay ikinglay ove:may
Utbay onay oremay eepday illway Iay endartay inemay eyeay
Anthay ouryay onsentcay ivesgay engthstray otay akemay itay flyay.

Enteray aay Ervantsay.

ERVANTsay.
Adammay, ethay uestsgay areay omecay, uppersay ervedsay upay, ouyay alledcay, myay oungyay adylay
askeday orfay, ethay Ursenay ursedcay inay ethay antrypay, anday everythingay inay extremityay.
Iay ustmay encehay otay aitway, Iay eseechbay ouyay ollowfay aightstray.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Eway ollowfay eethay.

Exit[_ay Ervant_]say.

Ulietjay, ethay Ountycay aysstay.

URSEnay.
Ogay, irlgay, eeksay appyhay ightsnay otay appyhay aysday.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IVay. Aay Eetstray.

Enteray Omeoray, Ercutiomay, Envoliobay, ithway ivefay oray ixsay Askers;may
Orch-bearerstay anday othersay.

OMEOray.
Atwhay, allshay isthay eechspay ebay okespay orfay ouray excuse?ay
Oray allshay eway onay ithoutway apology?ay

ENVOLIObay.
Ethay ateday isay outay ofay uchsay olixity:pray
E’llway avehay onay Upidcay oodwink’dhay ithway aay arfscay,
Earingbay aay Artar’stay aintedpay owbay ofay athlay,
Aringscay ethay adieslay ikelay aay ow-keeper;cray
Ornay onay ithout-bookway ologuepray, aintlyfay okespay
Afteray ethay ompterpray, orfay ouray entrance:ay
Utbay etlay emthay easuremay usay byay atwhay eythay illway,
E’llway easuremay emthay aay easuremay, anday ebay onegay.

OMEOray.
Ivegay emay aay orchtay, Iay amay otnay orfay isthay ambling;ay
Eingbay utbay eavyhay Iay illway earbay ethay ightlay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Aynay, entlegay Omeoray, eway ustmay avehay ouyay anceday.

OMEOray.
Otnay Iay, elievebay emay, ouyay avehay ancingday oesshay,
Ithway imblenay olessay, Iay avehay aay oulsay ofay eadlay
Osay akesstay emay otay ethay oundgray Iay annotcay ovemay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Ouyay areay aay overlay, orrowbay Upid’scay ingsway,
Anday oarsay ithway emthay aboveay aay ommoncay oundbay.

OMEOray.
Iay amay ootay oresay enpierceday ithway ishay aftshay
Otay oarsay ithway ishay ightlay eathersfay, anday osay oundbay,
Iay annotcay oundbay aay itchpay aboveay ullday oeway.
Underay ove’slay eavyhay urdenbay oday Iay inksay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Anday, otay inksay inay itay, ouldshay ouyay urdenbay ove;lay
Ootay eatgray oppressionay orfay aay endertay ingthay.

OMEOray.
Isay ovelay aay endertay ing?thay Itay isay ootay oughray,
Ootay uderay, ootay oisterous;bay anday itay ickspray ikelay ornthay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Ifay ovelay ebay oughray ithway ouyay, ebay oughray ithway ove;lay
Ickpray ovelay orfay ickingpray, anday ouyay eatbay ovelay ownday.
Ivegay emay aay asecay otay utpay myay isagevay in:ay utting[_Pay onay aay ask_]may.
Aay isorvay orfay aay isorvay. Atwhay arecay Iay
Atwhay uriouscay eyeay othday uoteqay eformities?day
Erehay areay ethay eetle-browsbay allshay ushblay orfay emay.

ENVOLIObay.
Omecay, ockknay anday enter;ay anday onay oonersay inay
Utbay everyay anmay etakebay imhay otay ishay egslay.

OMEOray.
Aay orchtay orfay e:may etlay antonsway, ightlay ofay earthay,
Ickletay ethay enselesssay ushesray ithway eirthay eels;hay
Orfay Iay amay overb’dpray ithway aay andsiregray asephray,
I’llay ebay aay andle-holdercay anday ooklay onay,
Ethay amegay asway e’ernay osay airfay, anday Iay amay oneday.

ERCUTIOmay.
Uttay, un’sday ethay ousemay, ethay onstable’scay ownay ord:way
Ifay outhay artay unday, e’llway awdray eethay omfray ethay iremay
Oray avesay ouryay everenceray ovelay, ereinwhay outhay ickeststay
Upay otay ethay earsay. Omecay, eway urnbay aylightday, ohay.

OMEOray.
Aynay, at’sthay otnay osay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Iay eanmay irsay, inay elayday
Eway asteway ouray ightslay inay ainvay, ightlay ightslay byay ayday.
Aketay ouray oodgay eaningmay, orfay ouray udgmentjay itssay
Ivefay imestay inay atthay ereay onceay inay ouray ivefay itsway.

OMEOray.
Anday eway eanmay ellway inay oinggay otay isthay ask;may
Utbay is’tay onay itway otay ogay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Whyay, aymay oneay ask?ay

OMEOray.

ERCUTIOmay.
Oay, enthay, Iay eesay Ueenqay Abmay athhay eenbay ithway ouyay.
Eshay isay ethay airies’fay idwifemay, anday eshay omescay
Inay apeshay onay iggerbay anthay anay agate-stoneay
Onay ethay ore-fingerfay ofay anay aldermanay,
Awndray ithway aay eamtay ofay ittlelay atomiesay
Overay en’smay osesnay asay eythay ielay asleep:ay
Erhay aggon-spokesway ademay ofay onglay inners’spay egs;lay
Ethay overcay, ofay ethay ingsway ofay asshoppers;gray
Erhay acestray, ofay ethay allestsmay ider’sspay eb;way
Ethay ollarscay, ofay ethay oonshine’smay ateryway eams;bay
Erhay ipwhay ofay icket’scray one;bay ethay ashlay, ofay ilm;fay
Erhay aggonerway, aay allsmay ey-coatedgray atgnay,
Otnay alfhay osay igbay asay aay oundray ittlelay ormway
Ick’dpray omfray ethay azylay ingerfay ofay aay aid:may
Erhay ariotchay isay anay emptyay azelnuthay,
Ademay byay ethay oinerjay uirrelsqay oray olday ubgray,
Imetay outay o’ay indmay ethay airies’fay oachmakerscay.
Anday inay isthay atestay eshay allopsgay ightnay byay ightnay
Oughthray overs’lay ainsbray, anday enthay eythay eamdray ofay ove;lay
O’eray ourtiers’cay eesknay, atthay eamdray onay urtsiescay aight;stray
O’eray awyers’lay ingersfay, owhay aightstray eamdray onay ees;fay
O’eray adies’lay ipslay, owhay aightstray onay isseskay eamdray,
Ichwhay oftay ethay angryay Abmay ithway istersblay aguesplay,
Ecausebay eirthay eathsbray ithway eetmeatssway aintedtay are:ay
Ometimesay eshay allopsgay o’eray aay ourtier’scay osenay,
Anday enthay eamsdray ehay ofay ellingsmay outay aay uit;say
Anday ometimesay omescay eshay ithway aay ithe-pig’stay ailtay,
Icklingtay aay arson’spay osenay asay aay ieslay asleepay,
Enthay eamsdray ehay ofay anotheray enefice:bay
Ometimesay eshay ivethdray o’eray aay oldier’ssay ecknay,
Anday enthay eamsdray ehay ofay uttingcay oreignfay oatsthray,
Ofay eachesbray, ambuscadosay, Anishspay adesblay,
Ofay ealthshay ivefay athomfay eep;day anday enthay anonay
Umsdray inay ishay earay, atay ichwhay ehay artsstay anday akes;way
Anday, eingbay usthay ightedfray, earssway aay ayerpray oray otway,
Anday eepsslay againay. Isthay isay atthay eryvay Abmay
Atthay atsplay ethay anesmay ofay orseshay inay ethay ight;nay
Anday akesbay ethay elf-locksay inay oulfay uttishslay airshay,
Ichwhay, onceay untangleday, uchmay isfortunemay odes:bay
Isthay isay ethay aghay, enwhay aidsmay ielay onay eirthay acksbay,
Atthay essespray emthay, anday earnslay emthay irstfay otay earbay,
Akingmay emthay omenway ofay oodgay arriage:cay

Ichwhay areay ethay ildrenchay ofay anay idleay ainbray,

Ichwhay isay asay inthay ofay ubstancesay asay ethay airay,
Anday oremay inconstantay anthay ethay indway, owhay oosway
Evenay ownay ethay ozenfray osombay ofay ethay orthnay,

ACTay IIay


Enteray Oruschay.

ORUScHay.
Ownay olday esireday othday inay ishay eathbedday ielay,
Anday oungyay affectionay apesgay otay ebay ishay eir;hay
Atthay airfay orfay ichwhay ovelay oan’dgray orfay anday ouldway ieday,
Ithway endertay Ulietjay atch’dmay, isay ownay otnay airfay.
Ownay Omeoray isay elov’dbay, anday oveslay againay,
Alikeay ewitchedbay byay ethay armchay ofay ooks;lay
Utbay otay ishay oefay uppos’dsay ehay ustmay omplaincay,
Anday eshay ealstay ove’slay eetsway aitbay omfray earfulfay ooks:hay
Eingbay eldhay aay oefay, ehay aymay otnay avehay accessay
Otay eathebray uchsay owsvay asay overslay useay otay ear;sway
Anday eshay asay uchmay inay ovelay, erhay eansmay uchmay esslay
Otay eetmay erhay ewnay elovedbay anywhereay.
Utbay assionpay endslay emthay owerpay, imetay eansmay, otay eetmay,
Emperingtay extremitiesay ithway extremeay eetsway.

Exit_][_ay.

Omeo!ray Umours!hay Adman!may Assion!pay Over!lay
Appearay outhay inay ethay ikenesslay ofay aay ighsay,

Anday ethay emesnesday atthay erethay adjacentay ielay,
Atthay inay thyay ikenesslay outhay appearay otay usay.

Isthay annotcay angeray imhay. ould’Tway angeray imhay

Ofay omesay angestray aturenay, ettinglay itay erethay andstay

Isay airfay anday onesthay, anday, inay ishay istress’may amenay,
Iay onjurecay onlyay utbay otay aiseray upay imhay.

ENVOLIObay.

Otay ebay onsortedcay ithway ethay umoroushay ightnay.
Indblay isay ishay ovelay, anday estbay efitsbay ethay arkday.

ERCUTIOmay.

Ownay illway ehay itsay underay aay edlarmay eetray,

Oay Omeoray, atthay eshay ereway, Oay atthay eshay ereway
Anay open-arseay anday outhay aay operinpay ear!pay

ENVOLIObay.

ENEsCay IIay. Apulet’scay Ardengay.

Enteray Omeoray.

OMEOray.
Ehay estsjay atay arsscay atthay evernay eltfay aay oundway.

Ulietjay appearsay aboveay atay aay indowway.

Utbay oftsay, atwhay ightlay oughthray onderyay indowway eaks?bray
Itay isay ethay eastay, anday Ulietjay isay ethay un!say
Ariseay airfay unsay anday illkay ethay enviousay oonmay,
Owhay isay alreadyay icksay anday alepay ithway iefgray,
Atthay outhay erhay aidmay artay arfay oremay airfay anthay eshay.
Ebay otnay erhay aidmay incesay eshay isay envious;ay
Erhay estalvay iverylay isay utbay icksay anday eengray,
Anday onenay utbay oolsfay oday earway it;ay astcay itay offay.
Itay isay myay adylay, Oay itay isay myay ove!lay
Oay, atthay eshay ewknay eshay ere!way
Eshay eaksspay, etyay eshay ayssay othingnay. Atwhay ofay at?thay
Erhay eyeay iscoursesday, Iay illway answeray itay.
Iay amay ootay oldbay, is’tay otnay otay emay eshay eaksspay.
Otway ofay ethay airestfay arsstay inay allay ethay eavenhay,
Avinghay omesay usinessbay, oday entreatay erhay eyesay
Otay inkletway inay eirthay eressphay illtay eythay eturnray.
Atwhay ifay erhay eyesay ereway erethay, eythay inay erhay ead?hay
Ethay ightnessbray ofay erhay eekchay ouldway ameshay osethay arsstay,
Asay aylightday othday aay amp;lay erhay eyesay inay eavenhay
Ouldway oughthray ethay airyay egionray eamstray osay ightbray
Atthay irdsbay ouldway ingsay anday inkthay itay ereway otnay ightnay.
Eesay owhay eshay eanslay erhay eekchay uponay erhay andhay.
Oay atthay Iay ereway aay oveglay uponay atthay andhay,
Atthay Iay ightmay ouchtay atthay eekchay.

ULIETjay.
Ayay emay.

OMEOray.
Eshay eaksspay.
Oay eakspay againay ightbray angelay, orfay outhay artay
Asay oriousglay otay isthay ightnay, eingbay o’eray myay eadhay,
Asay isay aay ingedway essengermay ofay eavenhay
Untoay ethay ite-upturnedwhay onderingway eyesay
Ofay ortalsmay atthay allfay ackbay otay azegay onay imhay
Enwhay ehay estridesbay ethay azy-puffinglay oudsclay
Anday ailssay uponay ethay osombay ofay ethay airay.

Oay Omeoray, Omeoray, ereforewhay artay outhay Omeo?ray
Enyday thyay atherfay anday efuseray thyay amenay.
Oray ifay outhay iltway otnay, ebay utbay ornsway myay ovelay,
Anday I’llay onay ongerlay ebay aay Apuletcay.

Aside_][_ay. Allshay Iay earhay oremay, oray allshay Iay eakspay atay is?thay

ULIETjay.
is’Tay utbay thyay amenay atthay isay myay enemy;ay
Outhay artay elfthysay, oughthay otnay aay Ontaguemay.
At’swhay Ontague?may Itay isay ornay andhay ornay ootfay,
Ornay armay, ornay acefay, ornay anyay otheray artpay
Elongingbay otay aay anmay. Oay ebay omesay otheray amenay.
At’swhay inay aay ame?nay Atthay ichwhay eway allcay aay oseray
Byay anyay otheray amenay ouldway ellsmay asay eet;sway
Osay Omeoray ouldway, ereway ehay otnay Omeoray all’dcay,
Etainray atthay earday erfectionpay ichwhay ehay owesay
Ithoutway atthay itletay. Omeoray, offday thyay amenay,
Anday orfay thyay amenay, ichwhay isay onay artpay ofay eethay,
Aketay allay elfmysay.

OMEOray.
Iay aketay eethay atay thyay ordway.
Allcay emay utbay ovelay, anday I’llay ebay ewnay aptis’d;bay
Enceforthhay Iay evernay illway ebay Omeoray.

Iay owknay otnay owhay otay elltay eethay owhay Iay am:ay

Ecausebay itay isay anay enemyay otay eethay.
Adhay Iay itay ittenwray, Iay ouldway eartay ethay ordway.

Artay outhay otnay Omeoray, anday aay Ontague?may

Eithernay, airfay aidmay, ifay eitheray eethay islikeday.

ENEsCay IIIay. Iarfray Awrence’slay Ellcay.

Enteray Iarfray Awrencelay ithway aay asketbay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Ethay ey-ey’dgray ornmay ilessmay onay ethay owningfray ightnay,
Equeringchay ethay easternay oudsclay ithway eaksstray ofay ight;lay
Anday eckledflay arknessday ikelay aay unkarddray eelsray
Omfray orthfay ay’sday athwaypay, ademay byay Itan’stay ieryfay eelswhay
Ownay, ereay ethay unsay advanceay ishay urningbay eyeay,
Ethay ayday otay eerchay, anday ight’snay ankday ewday otay dryay,
Iay ustmay upfillay isthay osieray agecay ofay oursay
Ithway alefulbay eedsway anday ecious-juicedpray owersflay.
Ethay earthay at’sthay ature’snay othermay, isay erhay omb;tay
Atwhay isay erhay uryingbay avegray, atthay isay erhay omb:way
Anday omfray erhay ombway ildrenchay ofay iversday indkay
Eway uckingsay onay erhay aturalnay osombay indfay.
Anymay orfay anymay irtuesvay excellentay,
Onenay utbay orfay omesay, anday etyay allay ifferentday.
Oay, icklemay isay ethay owerfulpay acegray atthay ieslay
Inay antsplay, erbshay, onesstay, anday eirthay uetray ualitiesqay.
Orfay aughtnay osay ilevay atthay onay ethay earthay othday ivelay
Utbay otay ethay earthay omesay ecialspay oodgay othday ive;gay
Ornay aughtay osay oodgay utbay, ain’dstray omfray atthay airfay useay,
Evoltsray omfray uetray irthbay, umblingstay onay abuseay.
Irtuevay itselfay urnstay icevay eingbay isappliedmay,
Anday icevay ometime’ssay byay actionay ignifiedday.

Enteray Omeoray.

Ithinway ethay infantay indray ofay isthay eakway owerflay
Oisonpay athhay esidenceray, anday edicinemay ower:pay
Orfay isthay, eingbay eltsmay, ithway atthay artpay eerschay eachay art;pay
Eingbay astedtay, aysslay allay ensessay ithway ethay earthay.
Otway uchsay opposeday ingskay encampay emthay illstay
Inay anmay asay ellway asay erbs—gracehay, anday uderay ill;way
Anday erewhay ethay orserway isay edominantpray,
Ullfay oonsay ethay ankercay eathday eatsay upay atthay antplay.

OMEOray.
Oodgay orrowmay, atherfay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Enedicite!bay
Atwhay earlyay onguetay osay eetsway alutethsay e?may
Oungyay onsay, itay arguesay aay istemper’dday eadhay
Osay oonsay otay idbay oodgay orrowmay otay thyay edbay.
Arecay eepskay ishay atchway inay everyay olday an’smay eyeay,
Anday erewhay arecay odgeslay eepslay illway evernay ie;lay
Utbay erewhay unbruiseday outhyay ithway unstuff’day ainbray
Othday ouchcay ishay imbslay, erethay oldengay eepslay othday eignray.
Ereforethay thyay earlinessay othday emay assureay
Outhay artay uprous’day ithway omesay istemperature;day

At’sthay myay oodgay onsay. Utbay erewhay asthay outhay eenbay en?thay

OMEOray.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.

OMEOray.
Enthay ainlyplay owknay myay eart’shay earday ovelay isay etsay

Asay inemay onay ershay, osay ershay isay etsay onay ine;may
Anday allay ombin’dcay, avesay atwhay outhay ustmay ombinecay
Byay olyhay arriagemay. Enwhay, anday erewhay, anday owhay

I’llay elltay eethay asay eway ass;pay utbay isthay Iay aypray,
Atthay outhay onsentcay otay arrymay usay odaytay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Olyhay Aintsay Ancis!fray Atwhay aay angechay isay ere!hay
Isay Osalineray, atthay outhay idstday ovelay osay earday,
Osay oonsay orsaken?fay Oungyay en’smay ovelay enthay ieslay
Otnay ulytray inay eirthay eartshay, utbay inay eirthay eyesay.
Esujay Ariamay, atwhay aay ealday ofay inebray
Athhay ash’dway thyay allowsay eekschay orfay Osaline!ray
Owhay uchmay altsay aterway ownthray awayay inay asteway,
Otay easonsay ovelay, atthay ofay itay othday otnay astetay.
Ethay unsay otnay etyay thyay ighssay omfray eavenhay earsclay,
Thyay olday oansgray etyay ingray inay inemay ancientay earsay.
Olay erehay uponay thyay eekchay ethay ainstay othday itsay
Ofay anay olday eartay atthay isay otnay ash’dway offay etyay.
Ifay ereay outhay astway elfthysay, anday esethay oesway inethay,
Outhay anday esethay oesway ereway allay orfay Osalineray,
Anday artay outhay ang’d?chay Onouncepray isthay entencesay enthay,

OMEOray.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Otnay inay aay avegray

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oay, eshay ewknay ellway
Thyay ovelay idday eadray byay oteray, atthay ouldcay otnay ellspay.

OMEOray.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.

ENVOLIObay.

ERCUTIOmay.

ethay immortalay assadopay, ethay untopay eversoray, ethay ayhay.

Ethay at?whay

ULIETjay.
Ethay ockclay uckstray inenay enwhay Iay idday endsay ethay Ursenay,
Inay alfhay anay ourhay eshay omisedpray otay eturnray.
Erchancepay eshay annotcay eetmay imhay. At’sthay otnay osay.
Oay, eshay isay amelay. Ove’slay eraldshay ouldshay ebay oughtsthay,
Ichwhay entay imestay asterfay idesglay anthay ethay un’ssay eamsbay,
Ivingdray ackbay adowsshay overay oweringlay ills:hay
Ereforethay oday imble-pinion’dnay ovesday awdray ovelay,
Anday ereforethay athhay ethay ind-swiftway Upidcay ingsway.
Ownay isay ethay unsay uponay ethay ighmosthay illhay
Ofay isthay ay’sday ourneyjay, anday omfray inenay illtay elvetway
Isay eethray onglay ourshay, etyay eshay isay otnay omecay.
Adhay eshay affectionsay anday armway outhfulyay oodblay,
E’dshay ebay asay iftsway inay otionmay asay aay all;bay
Myay ordsway ouldway andybay erhay otay myay eetsway ovelay,
Anday ishay otay emay.
Utbay olday olksfay, anymay eignfay asay eythay ereway ead;day
Unwieldyay, owslay, eavyhay anday alepay asay eadlay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Osay ilesmay ethay eavenshay uponay isthay olyhay actay
Atthay after-hoursay ithway orrowsay idechay usay otnay.

OMEOray.
Amenay, amenay, utbay omecay atwhay orrowsay ancay,
Itay annotcay ountervailcay ethay exchangeay ofay oyjay
Atthay oneay ortshay inutemay ivesgay emay inay erhay ightsay.
Oday outhay utbay oseclay ouray andshay ithway olyhay ordsway,
Enthay ove-devouringlay eathday oday atwhay ehay areday,
Itay isay enoughay Iay aymay utbay allcay erhay inemay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Esethay iolentvay elightsday avehay iolentvay endsay,
Anday inay eirthay iumphtray ie;day ikelay irefay anday owderpay,
Ichwhay asay eythay isskay onsumecay. Ethay eetestsway oneyhay
Isay oathsomelay inay ishay ownay eliciousnessday,
Anday inay ethay astetay onfoundscay ethay appetiteay.
Ereforethay ovelay oderately:may onglay ovelay othday o;say
Ootay iftsway arrivesay asay ardytay asay ootay owslay.

Illway e’ernay earway outay ethay everlastingay intflay.
Aay overlay aymay estridebay ethay ossamersgay
Atthay idlesay inay ethay antonway ummersay airay
Anday etyay otnay all;fay osay ightlay isay anityvay.

ULIETjay.
Oodgay evenay otay myay ostlyghay onfessorcay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Omeoray allshay ankthay eethay, aughterday, orfay usay othbay.

ULIETjay.
Asay uchmay otay imhay, elseay isay ishay anksthay ootay uchmay.

OMEOray.
Ahay, Ulietjay, ifay ethay easuremay ofay thyay oyjay
Ebay eap’dhay ikelay inemay, anday atthay thyay illskay ebay oremay
Otay azonblay itay, enthay eetensway ithway thyay eathbray
Isthay eighbournay airay, anday etlay ichray usic’smay onguetay
Unfolday ethay imagin’day appinesshay atthay othbay
Eceiveray inay eitheray byay isthay earday encounteray.

ULIETjay.
Onceitcay oremay ichray inay attermay anthay inay ordsway,
Agsbray ofay ishay ubstancesay, otnay ofay ornamentay.
Eythay areay utbay eggarsbay atthay ancay ountcay eirthay orth;way
Utbay myay uetray ovelay isay owngray otay uchsay excessay,
Iay annotcay umsay upay umsay ofay alfhay myay ealthway.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Omecay, omecay ithway emay, anday eway illway akemay ortshay orkway,
Orfay, byay ouryay eaveslay, ouyay allshay otnay aystay aloneay
Illtay olyhay urchchay incorporateay otway inay oneay.

Exeunt_][_ay.




ACTay IIIay

ENEsCay Iay. Aay ublicpay Aceplay.


Enteray Ercutiomay, Envoliobay, Agepay anday Ervantssay.

ENVOLIObay.
Iay aypray eethay, oodgay Ercutiomay, et’slay etire:ray
Ethay ayday isay othay, ethay Apuletscay abroaday,
Anday ifay eway eetmay, eway allshay otnay apescay aay awlbray,
Orfay ownay esethay othay aysday, isay ethay admay oodblay irringstay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Outhay artay ikelay oneay ofay esethay ellowsfay atthay, enwhay ehay entersay ethay onfinescay ofay
aay averntay, apsclay emay ishay ordsway uponay ethay abletay, anday ayssay od‘Gay endsay emay onay
eednay ofay ee!’thay anday byay ethay operationay ofay ethay econdsay upcay awsdray imhay onay ethay
awerdray, enwhay indeeday erethay isay onay eednay.

ENVOLIObay.
Amay Iay ikelay uchsay aay ellow?fay

ERCUTIOmay.
Omecay, omecay, outhay artay asay othay aay Ackjay inay thyay oodmay asay anyay inay Italy;ay anday asay
oonsay ovedmay otay ebay oodymay, anday asay oonsay oodymay otay ebay ovedmay.

ENVOLIObay.
Anday atwhay o?tay

ERCUTIOmay.
Aynay, anay erethay ereway otway uchsay, eway ouldshay avehay onenay ortlyshay, orfay oneay ouldway
illkay ethay otheray. Ou?thay Whyay, outhay iltway uarrelqay ithway aay anmay atthay athhay aay
airhay oremay oray aay airhay esslay inay ishay eardbay anthay outhay asthay. Outhay iltway uarrelqay
ithway aay anmay orfay ackingcray utsnay, avinghay onay otheray easonray utbay ecausebay outhay
asthay azelhay eyesay. Atwhay eyeay utbay uchsay anay eyeay ouldway spyay outay uchsay aay uarrel?qay
Thyay eadhay isay asay ullfay ofay uarrelsqay asay anay eggay isay ullfay ofay eatmay, anday etyay thyay
eadhay athhay eenbay eatenbay asay addleay asay anay eggay orfay uarrellingqay. Outhay asthay
uarrelledqay ithway aay anmay orfay oughingcay inay ethay eetstray, ecausebay ehay athhay
akenedway thyay ogday atthay athhay ainlay asleepay inay ethay unsay. Idstday outhay otnay allfay
outay ithway aay ailortay orfay earingway ishay ewnay oubletday eforebay Easter?ay ithway
anotheray orfay ingtyay ishay ewnay oesshay ithway anay olday iband?ray Anday etyay outhay iltway
utortay emay omfray uarrelling!qay

ENVOLIObay.
Anday Iay ereway osay aptay otay uarrelqay asay outhay artay, anyay anmay ouldshay uybay ethay eefay
implesay ofay myay ifelay orfay anay ourhay anday aay uarterqay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Ethay eefay imple!say Oay imple!say

Enteray Alttybay anday othersay.

ENVOLIObay.
Byay myay eadhay, erehay omescay ethay Apuletscay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Byay myay eelhay, Iay arecay otnay.

ALTtYBay.

Entlemengay, ood-den:gay aay ordway ithway oneay ofay ouyay.

ordway anday aay owblay.

ALTtYBay.
Ouyay allshay indfay emay aptay enoughay otay atthay, irsay, anday ouyay illway ivegay emay
occasionay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Ouldcay ouyay otnay aketay omesay occasionay ithoutway iving?gay

ALTtYBay.
Ercutiomay, outhay onsortestcay ithway Omeoray.

ERCUTIOmay.
Onsort?cay Atwhay, ostday outhay akemay usay instrels?may Anday outhay akemay instrelsmay ofay
usay, ooklay otay earhay othingnay utbay iscordsday. Ere’shay myay iddlestickfay, ere’shay
atthay allshay akemay ouyay anceday. Oundszay, onsort!cay

ENVOLIObay.
Eway alktay erehay inay ethay ublicpay aunthay ofay enmay.
Eitheray ithdrawway untoay omesay ivatepray aceplay,
Anday easonray oldlycay ofay ouryay ievancesgray,
Oray elseay epart;day erehay allay eyesay azegay onay usay.

ERCUTIOmay.
En’smay eyesay ereway ademay otay ooklay, anday etlay emthay azegay.
Iay illway otnay udgebay orfay onay an’smay easureplay, Iay.

Enteray Omeoray.

ALTtYBay.
Ellway, eacepay ebay ithway ouyay, irsay, erehay omescay myay anmay.

ERCUTIOmay.

Arrymay, ogay eforebay otay ieldfay, e’llhay ebay ouryay ollower;fay
Ouryay orshipway inay atthay ensesay aymay allcay imhay anmay.

ALTtYBay.

Onay etterbay ermtay anthay is:thay Outhay artay aay illainvay.

OMEOray.
Alttybay, ethay easonray atthay Iay avehay otay ovelay eethay
Othday uchmay excuseay ethay appertainingay ageray
Otay uchsay aay eetinggray. Illainvay amay Iay one;nay
Ereforethay arewell;fay Iay eesay outhay ow’stknay emay otnay.

ALTtYBay.
Oybay, isthay allshay otnay excuseay ethay injuriesay
Atthay outhay asthay oneday emay, ereforethay urntay anday awdray.

OMEOray.
Iay oday otestpray Iay evernay injur’day eethay,
Utbay ovelay eethay etterbay anthay outhay anstcay eviseday
Illtay outhay altshay owknay ethay easonray ofay myay ovelay.
Anday osay oodgay Apuletcay, ichwhay amenay Iay endertay
Asay earlyday asay inemay ownay, ebay atisfiedsay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Oay almcay, ishonourableday, ilevay ubmission!say
aws_][_Dray. Allaay occatastay arriescay itay awayay.

Atwhay ouldstway outhay avehay ithway e?may

ALTtYBay.
awing_][_Dray. Iay amay orfay ouyay.

ey[_Thay ight_]fay.

Exit[_ay Age_]pay.

OMEOray.
Ouragecay, an;may ethay urthay annotcay ebay uchmay.

ERCUTIOmay.
Onay, is’tay otnay osay eepday asay aay ellway, ornay osay ideway asay aay urchchay oorday, utbay is’tay
enoughay, ill’tway ervesay. Askay orfay emay omorrowtay, anday ouyay allshay indfay emay aay
avegray anmay. Iay amay epperedpay, Iay arrantway, orfay isthay orldway. Aay agueplay o’ay othbay
ouryay ouseshay. Oundszay, aay ogday, aay atray, aay ousemay, aay atcay, otay atchscray aay anmay otay
eathday. Aay aggartbray, aay ogueray, aay illainvay, atthay ightsfay byay ethay ookbay ofay
arithmetic!—Whyay ethay evilday amecay ouyay etweenbay us?ay Iay asway urthay underay ouryay
armay.

OMEOray.
Iay oughtthay allay orfay ethay estbay.

ERCUTIOmay.

Oray Iay allshay aintfay. Aay agueplay o’ay othbay ouryay ouseshay.
Eythay avehay ademay orms’way eatmay ofay emay.
Iay avehay itay, anday oundlysay ootay. Ouryay ouses!hay

Exeunt[_ay Ercutiomay anday Envolio_]bay.

OMEOray.
Isthay entlemangay, ethay Ince’spray earnay allyay,
Myay eryvay iendfray, athhay otgay ishay ortalmay urthay
Inay myay ehalf;bay myay eputationray ain’dstay
Ithway Alt’stybay ander—Tybalt,slay, atthay anay ourhay
Athhay eenbay myay ousincay. Oay eetsway Ulietjay,
Thyay eautybay athhay ademay emay effeminateay
Anday inay myay empertay often’dsay alour’svay eelstay.

E-enterray Envoliobay.

ENVOLIObay.
Oay Omeoray, Omeoray, avebray Ercutio’smay eadday,
Atthay allantgay iritspay athhay aspir’day ethay oudsclay,
Ichwhay ootay untimelyay erehay idday ornscay ethay earthay.

OMEOray.
Isthay ay’sday ackblay atefay onay omay aysday othday epend;day
Isthay utbay eginsbay ethay oeway othersay ustmay enday.

E-enterray Alttybay.

ENVOLIObay.
Erehay omescay ethay uriousfay Alttybay ackbay againay.

OMEOray.
Againay inay iumphtray, anday Ercutiomay ain?slay
Awayay otay eavenhay espectiveray enitylay,
Anday ire-ey’dfay uryfay ebay myay onductcay ow!nay
Ownay, Alttybay, aketay ethay illain’‘vay ackbay againay
Atthay atelay outhay av’stgay emay, orfay Ercutio’smay oulsay
Isay utbay aay ittlelay ayway aboveay ouray eadshay,
Ayingstay orfay inethay otay eepkay imhay ompanycay.
Eitheray outhay oray Iay, oray othbay, ustmay ogay ithway imhay.

ALTtYBay.
Outhay etchedwray oybay, atthay idstday onsortcay imhay erehay,
Altshay ithway imhay encehay.

OMEOray.
Isthay allshay etermineday atthay.

ey[_Thay ight;fay Alttybay alls_]fay.

ENVOLIObay.

IRSTfay ITIZENcay.

Iay argechay eethay inay ethay Ince’spray amenay obeyay.

Enteray Incepray, attended;ay Ontaguemay, Apuletcay, eirthay Ivesway anday othersay.

INCEpRay.
Erewhay areay ethay ilevay eginnersbay ofay isthay ay?fray

ENVOLIObay.
Oay oblenay Incepray, Iay ancay iscoverday allay
Ethay unluckyay anagemay ofay isthay atalfay awlbray.
Erethay ieslay ethay anmay, ainslay byay oungyay Omeoray,
Atthay ewslay thyay insmankay, avebray Ercutiomay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Alttybay, myay ousin!cay Oay myay other’sbray ild!chay
Oay Ince!pray Oay usband!hay Oay, ethay oodblay isay ill’dspay
Ofay myay earday insman!kay Incepray, asay outhay artay uetray,
Orfay oodblay ofay oursay edshay oodblay ofay Ontaguemay.
Oay ousincay, ousincay.

INCEpRay.
Envoliobay, owhay eganbay isthay oodyblay ay?fray

ENVOLIObay.
Alttybay, erehay ainslay, omwhay Omeo’sray andhay idday ay;slay
Omeoray, atthay okespay imhay airfay, idbay imhay ethinkbay
Owhay icenay ethay uarrelqay asway, anday urg’day ithalway
Ouryay ighhay ispleasureday. Allay isthay uttereday
Ithway entlegay eathbray, almcay ooklay, eesknay umblyhay ow’dbay
Ouldcay otnay aketay ucetray ithway ethay unrulyay eensplay
Ofay Alttybay, eafday otay eacepay, utbay atthay ehay iltstay
Ithway iercingpay eelstay atay oldbay Ercutio’smay eastbray,
Owhay, allay asay othay, urnstay eadlyday ointpay otay ointpay,
Anday, ithway aay artialmay ornscay, ithway oneay andhay eatsbay
Oldcay eathday asideay, anday ithway ethay otheray endssay
Itay ackbay otay Alttybay, osewhay exterityday
Etortsray itay. Omeoray ehay iescray alouday,
old‘Hay, iends!fray Iendsfray, art!’pay anday iftersway anthay ishay onguetay,
Ishay agileay armay eatsbay ownday eirthay atalfay ointspay,
Anday ixt’tway emthay ushes;ray underneathay osewhay armay
Anay enviousay ustthray omfray Alttybay ithay ethay ifelay
Ofay outstay Ercutiomay, anday enthay Alttybay edflay.
Utbay byay anday byay omescay ackbay otay Omeoray,
Owhay adhay utbay ewlynay entertain’day evengeray,
Anday o’ttay eythay ogay ikelay ightning;lay orfay, ereay Iay
Ouldcay awdray otay artpay emthay asway outstay Alttybay ain;slay
Anday asay ehay ellfay idday Omeoray urntay anday flyay.
Isthay isay ethay uthtray, oray etlay Envoliobay ieday.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ehay isay aay insmankay otay ethay Ontaguemay.
Affectionay akesmay imhay alsefay, ehay eaksspay otnay uetray.
Omesay entytway ofay emthay oughtfay inay isthay ackblay ifestray,
Anday allay osethay entytway ouldcay utbay illkay oneay ifelay.
Iay egbay orfay usticejay, ichwhay outhay, Incepray, ustmay ive;gay
Omeoray ewslay Alttybay, Omeoray ustmay otnay ivelay.

INCEpRay.
Omeoray ewslay imhay, ehay ewslay Ercutiomay.
Owhay ownay ethay icepray ofay ishay earday oodblay othday owe?ay

ONTAGUEmay.
Otnay Omeoray, Incepray, ehay asway Ercutio’smay iend;fray
Ishay aultfay oncludescay utbay atwhay ethay awlay ouldshay enday,
Ethay ifelay ofay Alttybay.

INCEpRay.
Anday orfay atthay offenceay
Immediatelyay eway oday exileay imhay encehay.
Iay avehay anay interestay inay ouryay ate’shay oceedingpray,
Myay oodblay orfay ouryay uderay awlsbray othday ielay a-bleedingay.
Utbay I’llay amerceay ouyay ithway osay ongstray aay inefay
Atthay ouyay allshay allay epentray ethay osslay ofay inemay.
Iay illway ebay eafday otay eadingplay anday excuses;ay
Ornay earstay ornay ayerspray allshay urchasepay outay abusesay.
Ereforethay useay onenay. Etlay Omeoray encehay inay astehay,
Elseay, enwhay ehay isay oundfay, atthay ourhay isay ishay astlay.
Earbay encehay isthay odybay, anday attenday ouray illway.
Ercymay utbay urdersmay, ardoningpay osethay atthay illkay.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IIay. Aay Oomray inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.

Enteray Ulietjay.

ULIETjay.
Allopgay apaceay, ouyay iery-footedfay eedsstay,
Owardstay Oebus’phay odginglay. Uchsay aay aggonerway
Asay Aetonphay ouldway ipwhay ouyay otay ethay estway
Anday ingbray inay oudyclay ightnay immediatelyay.
Eadspray thyay oseclay urtaincay, ove-performinglay ightnay,
Atthay unaway’sray eyesay aymay inkway, anday Omeoray
Eaplay otay esethay armsay, untalk’day ofay anday unseenay.
Overslay ancay eesay otay oday eirthay amorousay itesray
Byay eirthay ownay eauties:bay oray, ifay ovelay ebay indblay,
Itay estbay agreesay ithway ightnay. Omecay, ivilcay ightnay,
Outhay ober-suitedsay atronmay, allay inay ackblay,
Anday earnlay emay owhay otay oselay aay inningway atchmay,
Ay’dplay orfay aay airpay ofay ainlessstay aidenhoodsmay.
Oodhay myay unmann’day oodblay, atingbay inay myay eekschay,
Ithway thyay ackblay antlemay, illtay angestray ovelay, owgray oldbay,
Inkthay uetray ovelay acteday implesay odestymay.
Omecay, ightnay, omecay Omeo;ray omecay, outhay ayday inay ight;nay
Orfay outhay iltway ielay uponay ethay ingsway ofay ightnay
Iterwhay anthay ewnay owsnay uponay aay aven’sray ackbay.
Omecay entlegay ightnay, omecay ovinglay ack-brow’dblay ightnay,
Ivegay emay myay Omeoray, anday enwhay Iay allshay ieday,
Aketay imhay anday utcay imhay outay inay ittlelay arsstay,
Anday ehay illway akemay ethay acefay ofay eavenhay osay inefay
Atthay allay ethay orldway illway ebay inay ovelay ithway ightnay,
Anday aypay onay orshipway otay ethay arishgay unsay.
Oay, Iay avehay oughtbay ethay ansionmay ofay aay ovelay,
Utbay otnay ossess’dpay it;ay anday oughthay Iay amay oldsay,
Otnay etyay enjoy’day. Osay edioustay isay isthay ayday
Asay isay ethay ightnay eforebay omesay estivalfay
Otay anay impatientay ildchay atthay athhay ewnay obesray
Anday aymay otnay earway emthay. Oay, erehay omescay myay Ursenay,
Anday eshay ingsbray ewsnay, anday everyay onguetay atthay eaksspay
Utbay Omeo’sray amenay eaksspay eavenlyhay eloquenceay.

Enteray Ursenay, ithway ordscay.

Ownay, Ursenay, atwhay ews?nay Atwhay asthay outhay ere?thay
Ethay ordscay atthay Omeoray idbay eethay etch?fay

URSEnay.
Ayay, ayay, ethay ordscay.

ows[_Thray emthay own_]day.

ULIETjay.
Ayay emay, atwhay ews?nay Whyay ostday outhay ingwray thyay ands?hay

URSEnay.
Ahay, ell-a-dayway, e’shay eadday, e’shay eadday, e’shay ead!day
Eway areay undoneay, adylay, eway areay undoneay.
Alackay ethay ayday, e’shay onegay, e’shay ill’dkay, e’shay eadday.

ULIETjay.
Ancay eavenhay ebay osay envious?ay

URSEnay.
Omeoray ancay,
Oughthay eavenhay annotcay. Oay Omeoray, Omeoray.
Owhay everay ouldway avehay oughtthay it?ay Omeo!ray

ULIETjay.
Atwhay evilday artay outhay, atthay ostday ormenttay emay us?thay
Isthay orturetay ouldshay ebay oar’dray inay ismalday ellhay.
Athhay Omeoray ainslay imself?hay Aysay outhay utbay Ayay,
Anday atthay arebay owelvay Iay allshay oisonpay oremay
Anthay ethay eath-dartingday eyeay ofay ockatricecay.
Iay amay otnay Iay ifay erethay ebay uchsay anay I;ay
Oray osethay eyesay utshay atthay akemay eethay answeray Ayay.
Ifay ehay ebay ainslay, aysay Ay;ay oray ifay otnay, Onay.
Iefbray oundssay etermineday ofay myay ealway oray oeway.

URSEnay.
Iay awsay ethay oundway, Iay awsay itay ithway inemay eyesay,
Odgay avesay ethay ark!—heremay onay ishay anlymay eastbray.
Aay iteouspay orsecay, aay oodyblay iteouspay orse;cay
Alepay, alepay asay ashesay, allay edaub’dbay inay oodblay,
Allay inay ore-bloodgay. Iay oundedsway atay ethay ightsay.

ULIETjay.
Oay, eakbray, myay earthay. Oorpay ankroutbay, eakbray atay onceay.
Otay isonpray, eyes;ay e’ernay ooklay onay ibertylay.
Ilevay earthay otay earthay esign;ray enday otionmay erehay,
Anday outhay anday Omeoray esspray oneay eavyhay ierbay.

URSEnay.
Oay Alttybay, Alttybay, ethay estbay iendfray Iay adhay.
Oay ourteouscay Alttybay, onesthay entleman!gay
Atthay everay Iay ouldshay ivelay otay eesay eethay eadday.

ULIETjay.
Atwhay ormstay isay isthay atthay owsblay osay ontrary?cay
Isay Omeoray aughter’dslay anday isay Alttybay ead?day
Myay earestday ousincay, anday myay earerday ord?lay
Enthay eadfuldray umpettray oundsay ethay eneralgay oomday,
Orfay owhay isay ivinglay, ifay osethay otway areay one?gay

URSEnay.
Alttybay isay onegay, anday Omeoray anishedbay,
Omeoray atthay ill’dkay imhay, ehay isay anishedbay.

ULIETjay.
Oay Od!gay Idday Omeo’sray andhay edshay Alt’stybay ood?blay

URSEnay.
Itay idday, itay id;day alasay ethay ayday, itay idday.

ULIETjay.
Oay erpentsay earthay, idhay ithway aay oweringflay ace!fay
Idday everay agondray eepkay osay airfay aay ave?cay
Eautifulbay anttyray, iendfay angelicalay,
Ove-feather’dday avenray, olvish-raveningway amb!lay
Espisedday ubstancesay ofay ivinestday ow!shay
Ustjay oppositeay otay atwhay outhay ustlyjay eem’stsay,
Aay amnedday aintsay, anay onourablehay illain!vay
Oay aturenay, atwhay adsthay outhay otay oday inay ellhay
Enwhay outhay idstday owerbay ethay iritspay ofay aay iendfay
Inay ortalmay aradisepay ofay uchsay eetsway esh?flay
Asway everay ookbay ontainingcay uchsay ilevay attermay
Osay airlyfay ound?bay Oay, atthay eceitday ouldshay elldway
Inay uchsay aay orgeousgay alacepay.

URSEnay.
Ere’sthay onay usttray,
Onay aithfay, onay onestyhay inay enmay. Allay erjur’dpay,
Allay orswornfay, allay aughtnay, allay issemblersday.
Ahay, ere’swhay myay an?may Ivegay emay omesay aquaay itaevay.
Esethay iefsgray, esethay oesway, esethay orrowssay akemay emay olday.
Ameshay omecay otay Omeoray.

ULIETjay.
Ister’dblay ebay thyay onguetay
Orfay uchsay aay ish!way Ehay asway otnay ornbay otay ameshay.
Uponay ishay owbray ameshay isay asham’day otay it;say
Orfay is’tay aay onethray erewhay onourhay aymay ebay own’dcray
Olesay onarchmay ofay ethay universalay earthay.
Oay, atwhay aay eastbay asway Iay otay idechay atay im!hay

URSEnay.
Illway ouyay eakspay ellway ofay imhay atthay ill’dkay ouryay ousin?cay

ULIETjay.
Allshay Iay eakspay illay ofay imhay atthay isay myay usband?hay
Ahay, oorpay myay ordlay, atwhay onguetay allshay oothsmay thyay amenay,
Enwhay Iay thyay ee-hours’thray ifeway avehay angledmay it?ay
Utbay ereforewhay, illainvay, idstday outhay illkay myay ousin?cay
Atthay illainvay ousincay ouldway avehay ill’dkay myay usbandhay.
Ackbay, oolishfay earstay, ackbay otay ouryay ativenay ingspray,
Ouryay ibutarytray opsdray elongbay otay oeway,
Ichwhay ouyay istakingmay offeray upay otay oyjay.
Myay usbandhay iveslay, atthay Alttybay ouldway avehay ainslay,
Anday Alt’stybay eadday, atthay ouldway avehay ainslay myay usbandhay.
Allay isthay isay omfort;cay ereforewhay eepway Iay en?thay
Omesay ordway erethay asway, orserway anthay Alt’stybay eathday,
Atthay urder’dmay emay. Iay ouldway orgetfay itay ainfay,
Utbay Oay, itay essespray otay myay emorymay
Ikelay amnedday uiltygay eedsday otay inners’say indsmay.
Alttybay isay eadday, anday Omeoray anishedbay.
Atthay anished’‘bay, atthay oneay ordway anished’‘bay,
Athhay ainslay entay ousandthay Altstybay. Alt’stybay eathday
Asway oeway enoughay, ifay itay adhay endeday erethay.
Oray ifay oursay oeway elightsday inay ellowshipfay,
Anday eedlynay illway ebay ank’dray ithway otheray iefsgray,
Whyay ollow’dfay otnay, enwhay eshay aidsay Alt’stybay eadday,
Thyay atherfay oray thyay othermay, aynay oray othbay,
Ichwhay odernmay amentationlay ightmay avehay ov’d?may
Utbay ithway aay ear-wardray ollowingfay Alt’stybay eathday,
omeo‘Ray isay anished’—tobay eakspay atthay ordway
Isay atherfay, othermay, Alttybay, Omeoray, Ulietjay,
Allay ainslay, allay eadday. Omeoray isay anishedbay,
Erethay isay onay enday, onay imitlay, easuremay, oundbay,
Inay atthay ord’sway eathday, onay ordsway ancay atthay oeway oundsay.
Erewhay isay myay atherfay anday myay othermay, Urse?nay

URSEnay.
Eepingway anday ailingway overay Alt’stybay orsecay.
Illway ouyay ogay otay em?thay Iay illway ingbray ouyay itherthay.

ULIETjay.
Ashway eythay ishay oundsway ithway earstay. Inemay allshay ebay entspay,
Enwhay eirsthay areay dryay, orfay Omeo’sray anishmentbay.
Aketay upay osethay ordscay. Oorpay opesray, ouyay areay eguil’dbay,
Othbay ouyay anday I;ay orfay Omeoray isay exil’day.
Ehay ademay ouyay orfay aay ighwayhay otay myay edbay,
Utbay Iay, aay aidmay, ieday aiden-widowedmay.
Omecay ordscay, omecay Ursenay, I’llay otay myay eddingway edbay,
Anday eathday, otnay Omeoray, aketay myay aidenheadmay.

URSEnay.
Iehay otay ouryay amberchay. I’llay indfay Omeoray
Otay omfortcay ouyay. Iay otway ellway erewhay ehay isay.
Arkhay eyay, ouryay Omeoray illway ebay erehay atay ightnay.
I’llay otay imhay, ehay isay idhay atay Awrence’lay ellcay.

ULIETjay.
Oay indfay imhay, ivegay isthay ingray otay myay uetray ightknay,
Anday idbay imhay omecay otay aketay ishay astlay arewellfay.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IIIay. Iarfray Awrence’slay ellcay.

Enteray Iarfray Awrencelay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Omeoray, omecay orth;fay omecay orthfay, outhay earfulfay anmay.
Afflictionay isay enanmour’day ofay thyay artspay
Anday outhay artay eddedway otay alamitycay.

Enteray Omeoray.

OMEOray.
Atherfay, atwhay ews?nay Atwhay isay ethay Ince’spray oom?day
Atwhay orrowsay avescray acquaintanceay atay myay andhay,
Atthay Iay etyay owknay ot?nay

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Ootay amiliarfay
Isay myay earday onsay ithway uchsay oursay ompanycay.
Iay ingbray eethay idingstay ofay ethay Ince’spray oomday.

OMEOray.
Atwhay esslay anthay oomsdayday isay ethay Ince’spray oom?day

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Aay entlergay udgmentjay anish’dvay omfray ishay ipslay,

OMEOray.
Ahay, anishment?bay Ebay ercifulmay, aysay eath;day
Orfay exileay athhay oremay errortay inay ishay ooklay,
Uchmay oremay anthay eathday. Oday otnay aysay anishmentbay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Encehay omfray Eronavay artay outhay anishedbay.
Ebay atientpay, orfay ethay orldway isay oadbray anday ideway.

OMEOray.
Erethay isay onay orldway ithoutway Eronavay allsway,
Utbay urgatorypay, orturetay, ellhay itselfay.
Encehay anishedbay isay anish’dbay omfray ethay orldway,
Anday orld’sway exileay isay eathday. Enthay anishedbay
Isay eathday isterm’dmay. Allingcay eathday anishedbay,
Outhay utt’stcay myay eadhay offay ithway aay oldengay axeay,
Anday ilestsmay uponay ethay okestray atthay urdersmay emay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oay eadlyday insay, Oay uderay unthankfulness!ay
Thyay aultfay ouray awlay allscay eathday, utbay ethay indkay Incepray,
Akingtay thyay artpay, athhay ush’dbray asideay ethay awlay,
Anday urn’dtay atthay ackblay ordway eathday otay anishmentbay.
Isthay isay earday ercymay, anday outhay ee’stsay itay otnay.

OMEOray.
is’Tay orturetay, anday otnay ercymay. Eavenhay isay erehay
Erewhay Ulietjay iveslay, anday everyay atcay anday ogday,
Anday ittlelay ousemay, everyay unworthyay ingthay,
Ivelay erehay inay eavenhay anday aymay ooklay onay erhay,
Utbay Omeoray aymay otnay. Oremay alidityvay,
Oremay onourablehay atestay, oremay ourtshipcay iveslay
Inay arrioncay iesflay anthay Omeoray. Eythay aymay eizesay
Onay ethay itewhay onderway ofay earday Uliet’sjay andhay,
Anday ealstay immortalay essingblay omfray erhay ipslay,
Owhay, evenay inay urepay anday estalvay odestymay
Illstay ushblay, asay inkingthay eirthay ownay isseskay insay.
Utbay Omeoray aymay otnay, ehay isay anishedbay.
Isthay aymay iesflay oday, enwhay Iay omfray isthay ustmay flyay.
Eythay areay eefray enmay utbay Iay amay anishedbay.
Anday ay’stsay outhay etyay atthay exileay isay otnay eath?day
Adsthay outhay onay oisonpay ix’dmay, onay arp-groundshay ifeknay,
Onay uddensay eanmay ofay eathday, oughthay e’ernay osay eanmay,
Utbay anishedbay otay illkay e?may Anished?bay
Oay Iarfray, ethay amnedday useay atthay ordway inay ellhay.
Owlinghay attendsay itay. Owhay asthay outhay ethay earthay,
Eingbay aay ivineday, aay ostlyghay onfessorcay,
Aay in-absolversay, anday myay iendfray ofess’dpray,
Otay anglemay emay ithway atthay ordway anished?bay

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Outhay ondfay admay anmay, earhay emay eakspay aay ittlelay,

OMEOray.
Oay, outhay iltway eakspay againay ofay anishmentbay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
I’llay ivegay eethay armouray otay eepkay offay atthay ordway,
Adversity’say eetsway ilkmay, ilosophyphay,
Otay omfortcay eethay, oughthay outhay artay anishedbay.

OMEOray.
Etyay anished?bay Anghay upay ilosophyphay.
Unlessay ilosophyphay ancay akemay aay Ulietjay,
Isplantday aay owntay, everseray aay Ince’spray oomday,
Itay elpshay otnay, itay evailspray otnay, alktay onay oremay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oay, enthay Iay eesay atthay admay enmay avehay onay earsay.

OMEOray.
Owhay ouldshay eythay, enwhay atthay iseway enmay avehay onay eyes?ay

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Etlay emay isputeday ithway eethay ofay thyay estateay.

OMEOray.
Outhay anstcay otnay eakspay ofay atthay outhay ostday otnay eelfay.
Ertway outhay asay oungyay asay Iay, Ulietjay thyay ovelay,
Anay ourhay utbay arriedmay, Alttybay urderedmay,
Otingday ikelay emay, anday ikelay emay anishedbay,
Enthay ightstmay outhay eakspay, enthay ightstmay outhay eartay thyay airhay,
Anday allfay uponay ethay oundgray asay Iay oday ownay,
Akingtay ethay easuremay ofay anay unmadeay avegray.

ocking[_Knay ithin_]way.

OMEOray.

ocking_][_Knay.

ithin_][_Way. Etlay emay omecay inay, anday ouyay allshay owknay myay erranday.

Elcomeway enthay.

Enteray Ursenay.

URSEnay.
Oay olyhay Iarfray, Oay, elltay emay, olyhay Iarfray,
Erewhay isay myay ady’slay ordlay, ere’swhay Omeo?ray

URSEnay.
Oay, ehay isay evenay inay myay istress’may asecay.
Ustjay inay erhay ase!cay Oay oefulway athy!sympay
Iteouspay edicamentpray. Evenay osay ieslay eshay,
Ubberingblay anday eepingway, eepingway anday ubberingblay.
