Orfay Uliet’sjay akesay, orfay erhay akesay, iseray anday andstay.
Whyay ouldshay ouyay allfay intoay osay eepday anay O?ay

OMEOray.
Ursenay.

URSEnay.
Ahay irsay, ahay irsay, eath’sday ethay enday ofay allay.

OMEOray.
Akestspay outhay ofay Uliet?jay Owhay isay itay ithway er?hay
Othday otnay eshay inkthay emay anay olday urderermay,
Ownay Iay avehay ain’dstay ethay ildhoodchay ofay ouray oyjay
Ithway oodblay emov’dray utbay ittlelay omfray erhay own?ay
Erewhay isay e?shay Anday owhay othday e?shay Anday atwhay ayssay
Myay onceal’dcay adylay otay ouray ancell’dcay ove?lay

URSEnay.
Oay, eshay ayssay othingnay, irsay, utbay eepsway anday eeps;way
Anday ownay allsfay onay erhay edbay, anday enthay artsstay upay,
Anday Alttybay allscay, anday enthay onay Omeoray iescray,
Anday enthay ownday allsfay againay.

OMEOray.
Asay ifay atthay amenay,
Otshay omfray ethay eadlyday evellay ofay aay ungay,
Idday urdermay erhay, asay atthay ame’snay ursedcay andhay
Urder’dmay erhay insmankay. Oay, elltay emay, Iarfray, elltay emay,
Inay atwhay ilevay artpay ofay isthay anatomyay
Othday myay amenay odge?lay Elltay emay, atthay Iay aymay acksay
Ethay atefulhay ansionmay.

awing[_Dray ishay ord_]sway.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oldhay thyay esperateday andhay.
Artay outhay aay an?may Thyay ormfay iescray outay outhay artay.
Thyay earstay areay omanishway, thyay ildway actsay enoteday
Ethay unreasonableay uryfay ofay aay eastbay.
Unseemlyay omanway inay aay eemingsay anmay,
Anday ill-beseemingay eastbay inay eemingsay oth!bay
Outhay asthay amaz’day emay. Byay myay olyhay orderay,
Iay oughtthay thyay ispositionday etterbay emper’dtay.
Asthay outhay ainslay Alt?tybay Iltway outhay ayslay elf?thysay
Anday ayslay thyay adylay, atthay inay thyay ifelay iveslay,
Byay oingday amnedday atehay uponay elf?thysay
Whyay ail’stray outhay onay thyay irthbay, ethay eavenhay anday earth?ay
Incesay irthbay, anday eavenhay anday earthay, allay eethray oday eetmay
Inay eethay atay once;ay ichwhay outhay atay onceay ouldstway oselay.
Iefay, iefay, outhay am’stshay thyay apeshay, thyay ovelay, thyay itway,
Ichwhay, ikelay aay usureray, abound’stay inay allay,
Anday usestay onenay inay atthay uetray useay indeeday
Ichwhay ouldshay edeckbay thyay apeshay, thyay ovelay, thyay itway.
Thyay oblenay apeshay isay utbay aay ormfay ofay axway,
Igressingday omfray ethay alourvay ofay aay an;may
Thyay earday ovelay ornsway utbay ollowhay erjurypay,
Illingkay atthay ovelay ichwhay outhay asthay ow’dvay otay erish;chay
Thyay itway, atthay ornamentay otay apeshay anday ovelay,
Isshapenmay inay ethay onductcay ofay emthay othbay,
Ikelay owderpay inay aay illessskay oldier’ssay askflay,
Isay etsay afireay byay inethay ownay ignoranceay,
Anday outhay ismember’dday ithway inethay ownay efenceday.
Atwhay, ouseray eethay, anmay. Thyay Ulietjay isay aliveay,
Orfay osewhay earday akesay outhay astway utbay atelylay eadday.
Erethay artay outhay appyhay. Alttybay ouldway illkay eethay,
Utbay outhay ew’stslay Alt;tybay erethay artay outhay appyhay.
Ethay awlay atthay eaten’dthray eathday ecomesbay thyay iendfray,
Anday urnstay itay otay exile;ay erethay artay outhay appyhay.
Aay ackpay ofay essingsblay ightlay uponay thyay ack;bay
Appinesshay ourtscay eethay inay erhay estbay array;ay
Utbay ikelay aay isshapedmay anday ullensay enchway,
Outhay utt’stpay upay thyay Ortunefay anday thyay ovelay.
Aketay eedhay, aketay eedhay, orfay uchsay ieday iserablemay.
Ogay, etgay eethay otay thyay ovelay asay asway ecreedday,
Ascenday erhay amberchay, encehay anday omfortcay erhay.
Utbay ooklay outhay aystay otnay illtay ethay atchway ebay etsay,
Orfay enthay outhay anstcay otnay asspay otay Antua;may
Erewhay outhay altshay ivelay illtay eway ancay indfay aay imetay
Otay azeblay ouryay arriagemay, econcileray ouryay iendsfray,
Egbay ardonpay ofay ethay Incepray, anday allcay eethay ackbay
Ithway entytway undredhay ousandthay imestay oremay oyjay
Anthay outhay ent’stway orthfay inay amentationlay.
Ogay eforebay, Ursenay. Ommendcay emay otay thyay adylay,
Anday idbay erhay astenhay allay ethay ousehay otay edbay,
Ichwhay eavyhay orrowsay akesmay emthay aptay untoay.
Omeoray isay omingcay.

URSEnay.
Oay Ordlay, Iay ouldcay avehay ay’dstay erehay allay ethay ightnay
Otay earhay oodgay ounselcay. Oay, atwhay earninglay is!ay
Myay ordlay, I’llay elltay myay adylay ouyay illway omecay.

OMEOray.
Oday osay, anday idbay myay eetsway eparepray otay idechay.

URSEnay.
Erehay irsay, aay ingray eshay idbay emay ivegay ouyay, irsay.
Iehay ouyay, akemay astehay, orfay itay owsgray eryvay atelay.

Exit_][_ay.

OMEOray.
Owhay ellway myay omfortcay isay eviv’dray byay isthay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Ogay encehay, oodgay ightnay, anday erehay andsstay allay ouryay ate:stay
Eitheray ebay onegay eforebay ethay atchway ebay etsay,
Oray byay ethay eakbray ofay ayday isguis’dday omfray encehay.
Ojournsay inay Antuamay. I’llay indfay outay ouryay anmay,
Anday ehay allshay ignifysay omfray imetay otay imetay
Everyay oodgay aphay otay ouyay atthay anceschay erehay.
Ivegay emay thyay and;hay is’tay ate;lay arewell;fay oodgay ightnay.

OMEOray.
Utbay atthay aay oyjay astpay oyjay allscay outay onay emay,
Itay ereway aay iefgray osay iefbray otay artpay ithway eethay.
Arewellfay.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IVay. Aay Oomray inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.

Enteray Apuletcay, Adylay Apuletcay anday Arispay.

APULETcay.
Ingsthay avehay allenfay outay, irsay, osay unluckilyay
Atthay eway avehay adhay onay imetay otay ovemay ouray aughterday.
Ooklay ouyay, eshay ov’dlay erhay insmankay Alttybay earlyday,
Anday osay idday Iay. Ellway, eway ereway ornbay otay ieday.
is’Tay eryvay ate;lay e’llshay otnay omecay ownday onighttay.
Iay omisepray ouyay, utbay orfay ouryay ompanycay,
Iay ouldway avehay eenbay abeday anay ourhay agoay.

ARISpay.
Esethay imestay ofay oeway afforday onay unetay otay ooway.
Adammay, oodgay ightnay. Ommendcay emay otay ouryay aughterday.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Iay illway, anday owknay erhay indmay earlyay omorrow;tay
Onighttay e’sshay ew’dmay upay otay erhay eavinesshay.

APULETcay.
Irsay Arispay, Iay illway akemay aay esperateday endertay
Ofay myay ild’schay ovelay. Iay inkthay eshay illway ebay ul’dray
Inay allay espectsray byay e;may aynay oremay, Iay oubtday itay otnay.
Ifeway, ogay ouyay otay erhay ereay ouyay ogay otay edbay,
Acquaintay erhay erehay ofay myay onsay Aris’pay ovelay,
Anday idbay erhay, arkmay ouyay emay, onay Ednesdayway extnay,
Utbay, oftsay, atwhay ayday isay is?thay

ARISpay.
Ondaymay, myay ordlay.

APULETcay.
Onday!may Ahay, a!hay Ellway, Ednesdayway isay ootay oonsay,
Aay Ursdaythay etlay itay e;bay aay Ursdaythay, elltay erhay,
Eshay allshay ebay arriedmay otay isthay oblenay earlay.
Illway ouyay ebay eady?ray Oday ouyay ikelay isthay aste?hay
E’llway eepkay onay eatgray ado—aay, iendfray oray otway,
Orfay, arkhay ouyay, Alttybay eingbay ainslay osay atelay,
Itay aymay ebay oughtthay eway eldhay imhay arelesslycay,
Eingbay ouray insmankay, ifay eway evelray uchmay.
Ereforethay e’llway avehay omesay alfhay aay ozenday iendsfray,
Anday erethay anay enday. Utbay atwhay aysay ouyay otay Ursday?thay

ARISpay.
Myay ordlay, Iay ouldway atthay Ursdaythay ereway omorrowtay.

APULETcay.
Ellway, etgay ouyay onegay. Aay Ursdaythay ebay itay enthay.
Ogay ouyay otay Ulietjay ereay ouyay ogay otay edbay,
Eparepray erhay, ifeway, againstay isthay eddingway ayday.
Arewellfay, myay ord—Lightlay. otay myay amberchay, o!hay
Aforeay emay, itay isay osay eryvay eryvay atelay atthay eway
Aymay allcay itay earlyay byay anday byay. Oodgay ightnay.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay Vay. Anay openay Allerygay otay Uliet’sjay Amberchay, overlookingay ethay Ardengay.

Enteray Omeoray anday Ulietjay.

ULIETjay.
Iltway outhay ebay one?gay Itay isay otnay etyay earnay ayday.
Itay asway ethay ightingalenay, anday otnay ethay arklay,
Atthay ierc’dpay ethay earfulfay ollowhay ofay inethay ear;ay
Ightlynay eshay ingssay onay ondyay omegranatepay eetray.
Elievebay emay, ovelay, itay asway ethay ightingalenay.

OMEOray.
Itay asway ethay arklay, ethay eraldhay ofay ethay ornmay,
Onay ightingalenay. Ooklay, ovelay, atwhay enviousay eaksstray
Oday acelay ethay everingsay oudsclay inay onderyay eastay.
Ight’snay andlescay areay urntbay outay, anday ocundjay ayday
Andsstay iptoetay onay ethay istymay ountainmay opstay.
Iay ustmay ebay onegay anday ivelay, oray aystay anday ieday.

ULIETjay.
Ondyay ightlay isay otnay aylightday, Iay owknay itay, Iay.
Itay isay omesay eteormay atthay ethay unsay exhalesay
Otay ebay otay eethay isthay ightnay aay orchbearertay
Anday ightlay eethay onay thyay ayway otay Antuamay.
Ereforethay aystay etyay, outhay eed’stnay otnay otay ebay onegay.

OMEOray.
Etlay emay ebay a’entay, etlay emay ebay utpay otay eathday,
Iay amay ontentcay, osay outhay iltway avehay itay osay.
I’llay aysay onyay eygray isay otnay ethay orning’smay eyeay,
is’Tay utbay ethay alepay eflexray ofay Ia’scynthay owbray.
Ornay atthay isay otnay ethay arklay osewhay otesnay oday eatbay
Ethay aultyvay eavenhay osay ighhay aboveay ouray eadshay.
Iay avehay oremay arecay otay aystay anthay illway otay ogay.
Omecay, eathday, anday elcomeway. Ulietjay illsway itay osay.
Owhay is’tay, myay oul?say Et’slay alktay. Itay isay otnay ayday.

ULIETjay.
Itay isay, itay is!ay Iehay encehay, ebay onegay, awayay.
Itay isay ethay arklay atthay ingssay osay outay ofay unetay,
Ainingstray arshhay iscordsday anday unpleasingay arpsshay.
Omesay aysay ethay arklay akesmay eetsway ivision;day
Isthay othday otnay osay, orfay eshay ividethday usay.
Omesay aysay ethay arklay anday oathedlay oadtay angechay eyesay.
Oay, ownay Iay ouldway eythay adhay ang’dchay oicesvay ootay,
Incesay armay omfray armay atthay oicevay othday usay affrayay,
Untinghay eethay encehay ithway unt’s-uphay otay ethay ayday.
Oay ownay ebay onegay, oremay ightlay anday ightlay itay owsgray.

OMEOray.
Oremay ightlay anday ightlay, oremay arkday anday arkday ouray oesway.

Enteray Ursenay.

URSEnay.
Adammay.

ULIETjay.
Urse?nay

URSEnay.
Ouryay adylay othermay isay omingcay otay ouryay amberchay.
Ethay ayday isay okebray, ebay aryway, ooklay aboutay.

Exit_][_ay.

ULIETjay.
Enthay, indowway, etlay ayday inay, anday etlay ifelay outay.

OMEOray.
Arewellfay, arewellfay, oneay isskay, anday I’llay escendday.

escends_][_Day.

ULIETjay.
Artay outhay onegay o?say Ovelay, ordlay, ayay usbandhay, iendfray,
Iay ustmay earhay omfray eethay everyay ayday inay ethay ourhay,
Orfay inay aay inutemay erethay areay anymay aysday.
Oay, byay isthay ountcay Iay allshay ebay uchmay inay earsyay
Ereay Iay againay eholdbay myay Omeoray.

OMEOray.
Arewell!fay
Iay illway omitay onay opportunityay
Atthay aymay onveycay myay eetingsgray, ovelay, otay eethay.

ULIETjay.
Oay inkestthay outhay eway allshay everay eetmay again?ay

OMEOray.
Iay oubtday itay otnay, anday allay esethay oesway allshay ervesay
Orfay eetsway iscoursesday inay ouray imetay otay omecay.

ULIETjay.
Oay Od!gay Iay avehay anay ill-diviningay oul!say
Ethinksmay Iay eesay eethay, ownay outhay artay osay owlay,
Asay oneay eadday inay ethay ottombay ofay aay ombtay.
Eitheray myay eyesightay ailsfay, oray outhay ook’stlay alepay.

OMEOray.
Anday usttray emay, ovelay, inay myay eyeay osay oday ouyay.
Dryay orrowsay inksdray ouray oodblay. Adieuay, adieuay.

Exit[_ay elow_]bay.

ULIETjay.
Oay Ortunefay, Ortune!fay Allay enmay allcay eethay icklefay,
Ifay outhay artay icklefay, atwhay ostday outhay ithway imhay
Atthay isay enown’dray orfay aith?fay Ebay icklefay, Ortune;fay
Orfay enthay, Iay opehay outhay iltway otnay eepkay imhay onglay
Utbay endsay imhay ackbay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
ithin_][_Way. Ohay, aughterday, areay ouyay up?ay

Owhay is’tay atthay alls?cay Isay itay myay adylay other?may

Enteray Adylay Apuletcay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Whyay, owhay ownay, Uliet?jay

ULIETjay.
Adammay, Iay amay otnay ellway.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Evermoreay eepingway orfay ouryay ousin’scay eath?day
Atwhay, iltway outhay ashway imhay omfray ishay avegray ithway ears?tay
Anday ifay outhay ouldstcay, outhay ouldstcay otnay akemay imhay ivelay.
Ereforethay avehay one:day omesay iefgray owsshay uchmay ofay ovelay,
Utbay uchmay ofay iefgray owsshay illstay omesay antway ofay itway.

ULIETjay.
Etyay etlay emay eepway orfay uchsay aay eelingfay osslay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Osay allshay ouyay eelfay ethay osslay, utbay otnay ethay iendfray
Ichwhay ouyay eepway orfay.

ULIETjay.
Eelingfay osay ethay osslay,
Iay annotcay oosechay utbay everay eepway ethay iendfray.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ellway, irlgay, outhay eep’stway otnay osay uchmay orfay ishay eathday
Asay atthay ethay illainvay iveslay ichwhay aughter’dslay imhay.

ULIETjay.
Atwhay illainvay, adam?may

ADYlay APULETcay.
Atthay amesay illainvay Omeoray.

ULIETjay.
Illainvay anday ehay ebay anymay ilesmay asunderay.
Odgay ardonpay imhay. Iay oday, ithway allay myay earthay.
Anday etyay onay anmay ikelay ehay othday ievegray myay earthay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Atthay isay ecausebay ethay aitortray urderermay iveslay.

ULIETjay.
Ayay adammay, omfray ethay eachray ofay esethay myay andshay.
Ouldway onenay utbay Iay ightmay engevay myay ousin’scay eathday.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Eway illway avehay engeancevay orfay itay, earfay outhay otnay.
Enthay eepway onay oremay. I’llay endsay otay oneay inay Antuamay,
Erewhay atthay amesay anish’dbay unagateray othday ivelay,
Allshay ivegay imhay uchsay anay unaccustom’day amdray
Atthay ehay allshay oonsay eepkay Alttybay ompany:cay
Anday enthay Iay opehay outhay iltway ebay atisfiedsay.

ULIETjay.
Indeeday Iay evernay allshay ebay atisfiedsay
Ithway Omeoray illtay Iay eholdbay im—dead—hay
Isay myay oorpay earthay osay orfay aay insmankay ex’dvay.
Adammay, ifay ouyay ouldcay indfay outay utbay aay anmay
Otay earbay aay oisonpay, Iay ouldway empertay itay,
Atthay Omeoray ouldshay uponay eceiptray ereofthay,
Oonsay eepslay inay uietqay. Oay, owhay myay earthay abhorsay
Otay earhay imhay am’dnay, anday annotcay omecay otay imhay,
Otay eakwray ethay ovelay Iay orebay myay ousincay
Uponay ishay odybay atthay athhay aughter’dslay imhay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Indfay outhay ethay eansmay, anday I’llay indfay uchsay aay anmay.
Utbay ownay I’llay elltay eethay oyfuljay idingstay, irlgay.

ULIETjay.
Anday oyjay omescay ellway inay uchsay aay eedynay imetay.
Atwhay areay eythay, Iay eseechbay ouryay adyship?lay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ellway, ellway, outhay asthay aay arefulcay atherfay, ild;chay
Oneay owhay otay utpay eethay omfray thyay eavinesshay,
Athhay ortedsay outay aay uddensay ayday ofay oyjay,
Atthay outhay expectsay otnay, ornay Iay ook’dlay otnay orfay.

ULIETjay.
Adammay, inay appyhay imetay, atwhay ayday isay at?thay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Arrymay, myay ildchay, earlyay extnay Ursdaythay ornmay
Ethay allantgay, oungyay, anday oblenay entlemangay,
Ethay Ountycay Arispay, atay Aintsay Eter’spay Urchchay,
Allshay appilyhay akemay eethay erethay aay oyfuljay idebray.

ULIETjay.
Ownay byay Aintsay Eter’spay Urchchay, anday Eterpay ootay,
Ehay allshay otnay akemay emay erethay aay oyfuljay idebray.
Iay onderway atay isthay astehay, atthay Iay ustmay edway
Ereay ehay atthay ouldshay ebay usbandhay omescay otay ooway.
Iay aypray ouyay elltay myay ordlay anday atherfay, adammay,
Iay illway otnay arrymay et;yay anday enwhay Iay oday, Iay earsway
Itay allshay ebay Omeoray, omwhay ouyay owknay Iay atehay,
Atherray anthay Arispay. Esethay areay ewsnay indeeday.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Erehay omescay ouryay atherfay, elltay imhay osay ourselfyay,
Anday eesay owhay ehay illway aketay itay atay ouryay andshay.

Enteray Apuletcay anday Ursenay.

APULETcay.
Enwhay ethay unsay etssay, ethay airay othday izzledray ew;day
Utbay orfay ethay unsetsay ofay myay other’sbray onsay
Itay ainsray ownrightday.
Owhay ow?nay Aay onduitcay, irl?gay Atwhay, illstay inay ears?tay
Evermoreay owering?shay Inay oneay ittlelay odybay
Outhay ounterfeitscay aay arkbay, aay easay, aay indway.
Orfay illstay thyay eyesay, ichwhay Iay aymay allcay ethay easay,
Oday ebbay anday owflay ithway ears;tay ethay arkbay thyay odybay isay,
Ailingsay inay isthay altsay oodflay, ethay indsway, thyay ighssay,
Owhay agingray ithway thyay earstay anday eythay ithway emthay,
Ithoutway aay uddensay almcay illway oversetay
Thyay empest-tossedtay odybay. Owhay ownay, ife?way
Avehay ouyay eliver’dday otay erhay ouray ecree?day

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ayay, ir;say utbay eshay illway onenay, eshay ivesgay ouyay anksthay.
Iay ouldway ethay oolfay ereway arriedmay otay erhay avegray.

APULETcay.
Oftsay. Aketay emay ithway ouyay, aketay emay ithway ouyay, ifeway.
Owhay, illway eshay one?nay Othday eshay otnay ivegay usay anks?thay
Isay eshay otnay oud?pray Othday eshay otnay ountcay erhay estblay,
Unworthyay asay eshay isay, atthay eway avehay oughtwray
Osay orthyway aay entlemangay otay ebay erhay idegroom?bray

ULIETjay.
Otnay oudpray ouyay avehay, utbay ankfulthay atthay ouyay avehay.
Oudpray ancay Iay evernay ebay ofay atwhay Iay ate;hay
Utbay ankfulthay evenay orfay atehay atthay isay eantmay ovelay.

APULETcay.
Owhay ownay, owhay ownay, opp’dchay ogic?lay Atwhay isay is?thay
Oudpray, anday, Iay ankthay ouyay, anday Iay ankthay ouyay ot;nay
Anday etyay otnay oudpray. Istressmay inionmay ouyay,
Ankthay emay onay ankingsthay, ornay oudpray emay onay oudspray,
Utbay ettlefay ouryay inefay ointsjay ainst’gay Ursdaythay extnay
Otay ogay ithway Arispay otay Aintsay Eter’spay Urchchay,
Oray Iay illway agdray eethay onay aay urdlehay itherthay.
Outay, ouyay een-sicknessgray arrion!cay Outay, ouyay aggage!bay
Ouyay allow-face!tay

APULETcay.
Anghay eethay oungyay aggagebay, isobedientday etch!wray
Iay elltay eethay at—getwhay, eethay otay urchchay aay Ursdaythay,
Oray evernay afteray ooklay emay inay ethay acefay.

Oay Odgay eyay ood-en!gay

Aymay otnay oneay eak?spay

Eacepay, ouyay umblingmay ool!fay
Utteray ouryay avitygray o’eray aay ossip’sgay owlbay,
Orfay erehay eway eednay itay otnay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ouyay areay ootay othay.

APULETcay.
Od’sgay eadbray, itay akesmay emay ad!may
Ayday, ightnay, ourhay, ideray, imetay, orkway, ayplay,
Aloneay, inay ompanycay, illstay myay arecay athhay eenbay
Otay avehay erhay atch’dmay, anday avinghay ownay ovidedpray
Aay entlemangay ofay oblenay arentagepay,
Ofay airfay emesnesday, outhfulyay, anday oblynay allieday,
Uff’dstay, asay eythay aysay, ithway onourablehay artspay,
Oportion’dpray asay one’say oughtthay ouldway ishway aay anmay,
Anday enthay otay avehay aay etchedwray ulingpay oolfay,
Aay iningwhay ammetmay, inay erhay ortune’sfay endertay,
Otay answeray, I’ll‘ay otnay edway, Iay annotcay ovelay,
Iay amay ootay oungyay, Iay aypray ouyay ardonpay e’may.
Utbay, anday ouyay illway otnay edway, I’llay ardonpay ouyay.
Azegray erewhay ouyay illway, ouyay allshay otnay ousehay ithway emay.
Ooklay o’ttay, inkthay on’tay, Iay oday otnay useay otay estjay.
Ursdaythay isay ear;nay aylay andhay onay earthay, adviseay.
Anday ouyay ebay inemay, I’llay ivegay ouyay otay myay iend;fray
Anday ouyay ebay otnay, anghay, egbay, arvestay, ieday inay ethay eetsstray,
Orfay byay myay oulsay, I’llay e’ernay acknowledgeay eethay,
Ornay atwhay isay inemay allshay evernay oday eethay oodgay.
Usttray o’ttay, ethinkbay ouyay, I’llay otnay ebay orswornfay.

ULIETjay.
Isay erethay onay itypay ittingsay inay ethay oudsclay,

ULIETjay.
Oay Od!gay Oay Ursenay, owhay allshay isthay ebay evented?pray
Myay usbandhay isay onay earthay, myay aithfay inay eavenhay.
Owhay allshay atthay aithfay eturnray againay otay earthay,
Unlessay atthay usbandhay endsay itay emay omfray eavenhay
Byay eavinglay earth?ay Omfortcay emay, ounselcay emay.
Alackay, alackay, atthay eavenhay ouldshay actisepray atagemsstray
Uponay osay oftsay aay ubjectsay asay elfmysay.
Atwhay ay’stsay ou?thay Asthay outhay otnay aay ordway ofay oy?jay
Omesay omfortcay, Ursenay.

URSEnay.
Aithfay, erehay itay isay.
Omeoray isay anished;bay anday allay ethay orldway otay othingnay
Atthay ehay aresday e’ernay omecay ackbay otay allengechay ouyay.
Oray ifay ehay oday, itay eedsnay ustmay ebay byay ealthstay.
Enthay, incesay ethay asecay osay andsstay asay ownay itay othday,
Iay inkthay itay estbay ouyay arriedmay ithway ethay Ountycay.
Oay, e’shay aay ovelylay entlemangay.
Omeo’sray aay ishcloutday otay imhay. Anay eagleay, adammay,
Athhay otnay osay eengray, osay uickqay, osay airfay anay eyeay
Asay Arispay athhay. Eshrewbay myay eryvay earthay,
Iay inkthay ouyay areay appyhay inay isthay econdsay atchmay,
Orfay itay excelsay ouryay irst:fay oray ifay itay idday otnay,
Ouryay irstfay isay eadday, oray ere’tway asay oodgay ehay ereway,
Asay ivinglay erehay anday ouyay onay useay ofay imhay.

ULIETjay.
Eakestspay outhay omfray thyay eart?hay

URSEnay.
Anday omfray myay oulsay ootay,
Oray elseay eshrewbay emthay othbay.

ULIETjay.
Amenay.

URSEnay.
At?whay

ULIETjay.

URSEnay.

ULIETjay.
Ancientay amnation!day Oay ostmay ickedway iend!fay
Isay itay oremay insay otay ishway emay usthay orswornfay,
Oray otay ispraiseday myay ordlay ithway atthay amesay onguetay

ACTay IVay

ENEsCay Iay. Iarfray Awrence’slay Ellcay.


Enteray Iarfray Awrencelay anday Arispay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Onay Ursdaythay, ir?say Ethay imetay isay eryvay ortshay.

ARISpay.
Myay atherfay Apuletcay illway avehay itay o;say
Anday Iay amay othingnay owslay otay ackslay ishay astehay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Ouyay aysay ouyay oday otnay owknay ethay ady’slay indmay.
Unevenay isay ethay ourse;cay Iay ikelay itay otnay.

ARISpay.
Immoderatelyay eshay eepsway orfay Alt’stybay eathday,
Anday ereforethay avehay Iay ittlelay alk’dtay ofay ove;lay
Orfay Enusvay ilessmay otnay inay aay ousehay ofay earstay.
Ownay, irsay, erhay atherfay ountscay itay angerousday
Atthay eshay oday ivegay erhay orrowsay osay uchmay ay;sway
Anday inay ishay isdomway, asteshay ouray arriagemay,
Otay opstay ethay inundationay ofay erhay earstay,
Ichwhay, ootay uchmay indedmay byay erselfhay aloneay,
Aymay ebay utpay omfray erhay byay ocietysay.
Ownay oday ouyay owknay ethay easonray ofay isthay astehay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Aside_][_ay. Iay ouldway Iay ewknay otnay whyay itay ouldshay ebay ow’d—slay.
Ooklay, irsay, erehay omescay ethay adylay owardtay myay ellcay.

Enteray Ulietjay.

ARISpay.
Appilyhay etmay, myay adylay anday myay ife!way

ULIETjay.
Atthay aymay ebay, irsay, enwhay Iay aymay ebay aay ifeway.

ARISpay.
Atthay aymay ebay, ustmay ebay, ovelay, onay Ursdaythay extnay.

ULIETjay.
Atwhay ustmay ebay allshay ebay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
At’sthay aay ertaincay exttay.

ARISpay.
Omecay ouyay otay akemay onfessioncay otay isthay ather?fay

ULIETjay.
Otay answeray atthay, Iay ouldshay onfesscay otay ouyay.

ARISpay.
Oday otnay enyday otay imhay atthay ouyay ovelay emay.

ULIETjay.
Iay illway onfesscay otay ouyay atthay Iay ovelay imhay.

ARISpay.
Osay illway eyay, Iay amay uresay, atthay ouyay ovelay emay.

ULIETjay.
Ifay Iay oday osay, itay illway ebay ofay oremay icepray,
Eingbay okespay ehindbay ouryay ackbay anthay otay ouryay acefay.

ARISpay.
Oorpay oulsay, thyay acefay isay uchmay abus’day ithway earstay.

ULIETjay.
Ethay earstay avehay otgay allsmay ictoryvay byay at;thay
Orfay itay asway adbay enoughay eforebay eirthay itespay.

ARISpay.
Outhay ong’stwray itay oremay anthay earstay ithway atthay eportray.

ULIETjay.
Atthay isay onay anderslay, irsay, ichwhay isay aay uthtray,
Anday atwhay Iay akespay, Iay akespay itay otay myay acefay.

ARISpay.
Thyay acefay isay inemay, anday outhay asthay ander’dslay itay.

ULIETjay.
Itay aymay ebay osay, orfay itay isay otnay inemay ownay.
Areay ouyay atay eisurelay, olyhay atherfay, ownay,
Oray allshay Iay omecay otay ouyay atay eveningay ass?may

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Myay eisurelay ervessay emay, ensivepay aughterday, ow—nay.
Myay ordlay, eway ustmay entreatay ethay imetay aloneay.

ARISpay.
Odgay ieldshay Iay ouldshay isturbday evotion!—day
Ulietjay, onay Ursdaythay earlyay illway Iay ouseray eyay,
Illtay enthay, adieu;ay anday eepkay isthay olyhay isskay.

Exit_][_ay.

ULIETjay.
Oay utshay ethay oorday, anday enwhay outhay asthay oneday osay,
Omecay eepway ithway emay, astpay opehay, astpay urecay, astpay elp!hay

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oay Ulietjay, Iay alreadyay owknay thyay ief;gray
Itay ainsstray emay astpay ethay ompasscay ofay myay itsway.
Iay earhay outhay ustmay, anday othingnay aymay oroguepray itay,
Onay Ursdaythay extnay ebay arriedmay otay isthay Ountycay.

ULIETjay.
Elltay emay otnay, Iarfray, atthay outhay ear’sthay ofay isthay,
Unlessay outhay elltay emay owhay Iay aymay eventpray itay.
Ifay inay thyay isdomway, outhay anstcay ivegay onay elphay,
Oday outhay utbay allcay myay esolutionray iseway,
Anday ithway isthay ifeknay I’llay elphay itay esentlypray.
Odgay oin’djay myay earthay anday Omeo’sray, outhay ouray ands;hay
Anday ereay isthay andhay, byay eethay otay Omeo’sray eal’dsay,
Allshay ebay ethay abellay otay anotheray eedday,
Oray myay uetray earthay ithway eacheroustray evoltray
Urntay otay anotheray, isthay allshay ayslay emthay othbay.
Ereforethay, outay ofay thyay ong-experienc’dlay imetay,
Ivegay emay omesay esentpray ounselcay, oray eholdbay
ixt’Tway myay extremesay anday emay isthay oodyblay ifeknay
Allshay ayplay ethay empireay, arbitratingay atthay
Ichwhay ethay ommissioncay ofay thyay earsyay anday artay
Ouldcay otay onay issueay ofay uetray onourhay ingbray.
Ebay otnay osay onglay otay eakspay. Iay onglay otay ieday,
Ifay atwhay outhay eak’stspay eakspay otnay ofay emedyray.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oldhay, aughterday. Iay oday spyay aay indkay ofay opehay,
Ichwhay avescray asay esperateday anay executionay
Asay atthay isay esperateday ichwhay eway ouldway eventpray.
Ifay, atherray anthay otay arrymay Ountycay Arispay
Outhay asthay ethay engthstray ofay illway otay ayslay elfthysay,
Enthay isay itay ikelylay outhay iltway undertakeay
Aay ingthay ikelay eathday otay idechay awayay isthay ameshay,
Atthay op’stcay ithway eathday imselfhay otay apescay omfray itay.
Anday ifay outhay ar’stday, I’llay ivegay eethay emedyray.

ULIETjay.
Oay, idbay emay eaplay, atherray anthay arrymay Arispay,
Omfray offay ethay attlementsbay ofay onderyay owertay,
Oray alkway inay ievishthay aysway, oray idbay emay urklay
Erewhay erpentssay areay. Ainchay emay ithway oaringray ears;bay
Oray idehay emay ightlynay inay aay arnel-housechay,
O’er-cover’day uiteqay ithway eadday en’smay attlingray onesbay,
Ithway eekyray anksshay anday ellowyay aplesschay ullsskay.
Oray idbay emay ogay intoay aay ew-madenay avegray,
Anday idehay emay ithway aay eadday anmay inay ishay oud;shray
Ingsthay atthay, otay earhay emthay oldtay, avehay ademay emay embletray,
Anday Iay illway oday itay ithoutway earfay oray oubtday,
Otay ivelay anay unstain’day ifeway otay myay eetsway ovelay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Oldhay enthay. Ogay omehay, ebay errymay, ivegay onsentcay
Otay arrymay Arispay. Ednesdayway isay omorrow;tay
Omorrowtay ightnay ooklay atthay outhay ielay aloneay,
Etlay otnay thyay Ursenay ielay ithway eethay inay thyay amberchay.
Aketay outhay isthay ialvay, eingbay enthay inay edbay,
Anday isthay istilledday iquorlay inkdray outhay offay,
Enwhay esentlypray oughthray allay thyay einsvay allshay unray
Aay oldcay anday owsydray umour;hay orfay onay ulsepay
Allshay eepkay ishay ativenay ogresspray, utbay urceasesay.
Onay armthway, onay eathbray allshay estifytay outhay ivestlay,
Ethay osesray inay thyay ipslay anday eekschay allshay adefay
Otay alypay ashes;ay thyay eyes’ay indowsway allfay,
Ikelay eathday enwhay ehay utsshay upay ethay ayday ofay ifelay.
Eachay artpay epriv’dday ofay upplesay overnmentgay,
Allshay iffstay anday arkstay anday oldcay appearay ikelay eathday.
Anday inay isthay orrow’dbay ikenesslay ofay unkshray eathday
Outhay altshay ontinuecay otway anday ortyfay ourshay,
Anday enthay awakeay asay omfray aay easantplay eepslay.
Ownay enwhay ethay idegroombray inay ethay orningmay omescay
Otay ouseray eethay omfray thyay edbay, erethay artay outhay eadday.
Enthay asay ethay annermay ofay ouray ountrycay isay,
Inay thyay estbay obesray, uncover’day, onay ethay ierbay,
Outhay altshay ebay ornebay otay atthay amesay ancientay aultvay
Erewhay allay ethay indredkay ofay ethay Apuletscay ielay.
Inay ethay eantimemay, againstay outhay altshay awakeay,
Allshay Omeoray byay myay etterslay owknay ouray iftdray,
Anday itherhay allshay ehay omecay, anday ehay anday Iay
Illway atchway thyay akingway, anday atthay eryvay ightnay
Allshay Omeoray earbay eethay encehay otay Antuamay.
Anday isthay allshay eefray eethay omfray isthay esentpray ameshay,
Ifay onay inconstantay oytay ornay omanishway earfay
Abateay thyay alourvay inay ethay actingay itay.

ULIETjay.
Ivegay emay, ivegay e!may Oay elltay otnay emay ofay ear!fay

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Old;hay etgay ouyay onegay, ebay ongstray anday osperouspray
Inay isthay esolveray. I’llay endsay aay iarfray ithway eedspay
Otay Antuamay, ithway myay etterslay otay thyay ordlay.

ULIETjay.
Ovelay ivegay emay engthstray, anday engthstray allshay elphay afforday.
Arewellfay, earday atherfay.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IIay. Allhay inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.

Enteray Apuletcay, Adylay Apuletcay, Ursenay anday Ervantssay.

APULETcay.
Osay anymay uestsgay inviteay asay erehay areay itwray.

Exit[_ay irstfay Ervant_]say.

Irrahsay, ogay irehay emay entytway unningcay ookscay.

ECONDsay ERVANTsay.
Ouyay allshay avehay onenay illay, ir;say orfay I’llay tryay ifay eythay ancay icklay eirthay
ingersfay.

APULETcay.
Owhay anstcay outhay tryay emthay o?say

ECONDsay ERVANTsay.
Arrymay, irsay, is’tay anay illay ookcay atthay annotcay icklay ishay ownay ingers;fay
ereforethay ehay atthay annotcay icklay ishay ingersfay oesgay otnay ithway emay.

APULETcay.
Ogay, egonebay.

Exit[_ay econdsay Ervant_]say.

Eway allshay ebay uchmay unfurnish’day orfay isthay imetay.
Atwhay, isay myay aughterday onegay otay Iarfray Awrence?lay

URSEnay.
Ayay, orsoothfay.

APULETcay.
Ellway, ehay aymay ancechay otay oday omesay oodgay onay erhay.
Aay eevishpay elf-will’dsay arlotryhay itay isay.

Enteray Ulietjay.

URSEnay.
Eesay erewhay eshay omescay omfray iftshray ithway errymay ooklay.

APULETcay.
Owhay ownay, myay eadstronghay. Erewhay avehay ouyay eenbay adding?gay

ULIETjay.
Erewhay Iay avehay earntlay emay otay epentray ethay insay
Ofay isobedientday oppositionay
Otay ouyay anday ouryay ehests;bay anday amay enjoin’day
Byay olyhay Awrencelay otay allfay ostratepray erehay,
Otay egbay ouryay ardonpay. Ardonpay, Iay eseechbay ouyay.
Enceforwardhay Iay amay everay ul’dray byay ouyay.

APULETcay.
Endsay orfay ethay Ountycay, ogay elltay imhay ofay isthay.
I’llay avehay isthay otknay itknay upay omorrowtay orningmay.

ULIETjay.
Iay etmay ethay outhfulyay ordlay atay Awrence’lay ellcay,
Anday avegay imhay atwhay ecomedbay ovelay Iay ightmay,
Otnay eppingstay o’eray ethay oundsbay ofay odestymay.

APULETcay.
Whyay, Iay amay adglay on’tay. Isthay isay ellway. Andstay upay.
Isthay isay as’tay ouldshay ebay. Etlay emay eesay ethay Ountycay.
Ayay, arrymay. Ogay, Iay aysay, anday etchfay imhay itherhay.
Ownay aforeay Odgay, isthay everendray olyhay Iarfray,
Allay ouray olewhay itycay isay uchmay oundbay otay imhay.

ULIETjay.
Ursenay, illway ouyay ogay ithway emay intoay myay osetclay,
Otay elphay emay ortsay uchsay eedfulnay ornamentsay
Asay ouyay inkthay itfay otay urnishfay emay omorrow?tay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Onay, otnay illtay Ursdaythay. Erethay isay imetay enoughay.

APULETcay.
Ogay, Ursenay, ogay ithway erhay. E’llway otay urchchay omorrowtay.

Exeunt[_ay Ulietjay anday Urse_]nay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Eway allshay ebay ortshay inay ouray ovisionpray,
is’Tay ownay earnay ightnay.

APULETcay.
Ushtay, Iay illway irstay aboutay,
Anday allay ingsthay allshay ebay ellway, Iay arrantway eethay, ifeway.
Ogay outhay otay Ulietjay, elphay otay eckday upay erhay.
I’llay otnay otay edbay onighttay, etlay emay aloneay.
I’llay ayplay ethay ousewifehay orfay isthay once—Whatay., o!—hay
Eythay areay allay orth:fay ellway, Iay illway alkway elfmysay
Otay Ountycay Arispay, otay eparepray imhay upay
Againstay omorrowtay. Myay earthay isay ondrousway ightlay
Incesay isthay amesay aywardway irlgay isay osay eclaim’dray.

Exeunt_][_ay.

ENEsCay IIIay. Uliet’sjay Amberchay.

Enteray Ulietjay anday Ursenay.

ULIETjay.
Ayay, osethay attiresay areay estbay. Utbay, entlegay Ursenay,
Iay aypray eethay eavelay emay otay elfmysay onight;tay
Orfay Iay avehay eednay ofay anymay orisonsay
Otay ovemay ethay eavenshay otay ilesmay uponay myay atestay,
Ichwhay, ellway outhay ow’stknay, isay osscray anday ullfay ofay insay.

Enteray Adylay Apuletcay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Atwhay, areay ouyay usybay, o?hay Eednay ouyay myay elp?hay

ULIETjay.
Onay, adam;may eway avehay ull’dcay uchsay ecessariesnay
Asay areay ehovefulbay orfay ouray atestay omorrowtay.
Osay easeplay ouyay, etlay emay ownay ebay eftlay aloneay,
Anday etlay ethay ursenay isthay ightnay itsay upay ithway ouyay,
Orfay Iay amay uresay ouyay avehay ouryay andshay ullfay allay
Inay isthay osay uddensay usinessbay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Oodgay ightnay.
Etgay eethay otay edbay anday estray, orfay outhay asthay eednay.

Exeunt[_ay Adylay Apuletcay anday Urse_]nay.

ULIETjay.
Arewellfay. Odgay owsknay enwhay eway allshay eetmay againay.
Iay avehay aay aintfay oldcay earfay illsthray oughthray myay einsvay
Atthay almostay eezesfray upay ethay eathay ofay ifelay.
I’llay allcay emthay ackbay againay otay omfortcay emay.
Urse!—Whatnay ouldshay eshay oday ere?hay
Myay ismalday enescay Iay eedsnay ustmay actay aloneay.
Omecay, ialvay.
Atwhay ifay isthay ixturemay oday otnay orkway atay all?ay
Allshay Iay ebay arriedmay enthay omorrowtay orning?may
Onay, O!nay Isthay allshay orbidfay itay. Ielay outhay erethay.

aying[_Lay ownday erhay agger_]day.

Atwhay ifay itay ebay aay oisonpay, ichwhay ethay Iarfray
Ubtlysay athhay inister’dmay otay avehay emay eadday,
Estlay inay isthay arriagemay ehay ouldshay ebay ishonour’dday,
Ecausebay ehay arriedmay emay eforebay otay Omeo?ray
Iay earfay itay isay. Anday etyay ethinksmay itay ouldshay otnay,
Orfay ehay athhay illstay eenbay iedtray aay olyhay anmay.
Owhay ifay, enwhay Iay amay aidlay intoay ethay ombtay,
Iay akeway eforebay ethay imetay atthay Omeoray
Omecay otay edeemray e?may Ere’sthay aay earfulfay oint!pay
Allshay Iay otnay enthay ebay ifledstay inay ethay aultvay,
Otay osewhay oulfay outhmay onay ealthsomehay airay eathesbray inay,
Anday erethay ieday angledstray ereay myay Omeoray omes?cay
Oray, ifay Iay ivelay, isay itay otnay eryvay ikelay,
Ethay orriblehay onceitcay ofay eathday anday ightnay,
Ogethertay ithway ethay errortay ofay ethay aceplay,
Asay inay aay aultvay, anay ancientay eceptacleray,
Erewhay orfay isthay anymay undredhay earsyay ethay onesbay
Ofay allay myay uriedbay ancestorsay areay ack’dpay,
Erewhay oodyblay Alttybay, etyay utbay eengray inay earthay,
Ieslay esteringfay inay ishay oud;shray erewhay, asay eythay aysay,
Atay omesay ourshay inay ethay ightnay iritsspay esort—ray
Alackay, alackay, isay itay otnay ikelay atthay Iay,
Osay earlyay akingway, atwhay ithway oathsomelay ellssmay,
Anday ieksshray ikelay andrakesmay orntay outay ofay ethay earthay,
Atthay ivinglay ortalsmay, earinghay emthay, unray admay.
Oay, ifay Iay akeway, allshay Iay otnay ebay istraughtday,
Environeday ithway allay esethay ideoushay earsfay,
Anday adlymay ayplay ithway myay orefathers’fay oints?jay
Anday uckplay ethay angledmay Alttybay omfray ishay oud?shray
Anday, inay isthay ageray, ithway omesay eatgray insman’skay onebay,
Asay ithway aay ubclay, ashday outay myay esperateday ains?bray
Oay ooklay, ethinksmay Iay eesay myay ousin’scay ostghay
Eekingsay outay Omeoray atthay idday itspay ishay odybay
Uponay aay apier’sray ointpay. Aystay, Alttybay, ay!stay
Omeoray, Omeoray, Omeoray, ere’shay ink!dray Iay inkdray otay eethay.

ows[_Thray erselfhay onay ethay ed_]bay.

ENEsCay IVay. Allhay inay Apulet’scay Ousehay.

URSEnay.
Eythay allcay orfay atesday anday uincesqay inay ethay astrypay.

Enteray Apuletcay.

APULETcay.
Omecay, irstay, irstay, ir!stay Ethay econdsay ockcay athhay ow’dcray,
Ethay urfewcay ellbay athhay ungray, is’tay eethray o’clockay.
Ooklay otay ethay ak’dbay eatsmay, oodgay Angelica;ay
Arespay otnay orfay ostcay.

URSEnay.
Ogay, ouyay ot-queancay, ogay,
Etgay ouyay otay ed;bay aithfay, ou’llyay ebay icksay omorrowtay
Orfay isthay ight’snay atchingway.

APULETcay.
Onay, otnay aay itwhay. At!whay Iay avehay atch’dway ereay ownay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Ayay, ouyay avehay eenbay aay ouse-huntmay inay ouryay ime;tay
Utbay Iay illway atchway ouyay omfray uchsay atchingway ownay.

Aay ealous-hoodjay, aay ealous-hood!jay

irrah—Say, etchfay ierdray ogslay.

ay[_Play usic_]may.

ENEsCay Vay. Uliet’sjay Amber;chay Ulietjay onay ethay edbay.

Enteray Ursenay.

URSEnay.
Istress!may Atwhay, istress!may Uliet!jay Astfay, Iay arrantway erhay, eshay.
Whyay, amblay, whyay, adylay, iefay, ouyay ug-abed!slay
Whyay, ovelay, Iay ay!say Adam!may Eetheart!sway Whyay, ide!bray
Atwhay, otnay aay ord?way Ouyay aketay ouryay ennyworthspay ownay.
Eepslay orfay aay eek;way orfay ethay extnay ightnay, Iay arrantway,
Ethay Ountycay Arispay athhay etsay upay ishay estray
Atthay ouyay allshay estray utbay ittlelay. Odgay orgivefay e!may
Arrymay anday amenay. Owhay oundsay isay eshay asleep!ay
Iay eedsnay ustmay akeway erhay. Adammay, adammay, adam!may
Ayay, etlay ethay Ountycay aketay ouyay inay ouryay edbay,
E’llhay ightfray ouyay upay, i’faithay. Illway itay otnay e?bay
Atwhay, ess’ddray, anday inay ouryay othesclay, anday ownday again?ay
Iay ustmay eedsnay akeway ouyay. Ady!lay Ady!lay Ady!lay
Alasay, alas!ay Elphay, elp!hay Myay ady’slay ead!day
Oay, ell-a-dayway atthay everay Iay asway ornbay.
Omesay aquaay itaevay, o!hay Myay ord!lay Myay ady!lay

Enteray Adylay Apuletcay.

ADYlay APULETcay.
Atwhay oisenay isay ere?hay

URSEnay.
Oay amentablelay ay!day

ADYlay APULETcay.
Atwhay isay ethay atter?may

URSEnay.
Ooklay, ook!lay Oay eavyhay ay!day

ADYlay APULETcay.
Oay emay, Oay e!may Myay ildchay, myay onlyay ifelay.
Eviveray, ooklay upay, oray Iay illway ieday ithway eethay.
Elphay, elp!hay Allcay elphay.

Enteray Apuletcay.

APULETcay.
Orfay ameshay, ingbray Ulietjay orthfay, erhay ordlay isay omecay.

URSEnay.
E’sshay eadday, eceas’dday, e’sshay ead;day alackay ethay ay!day

ADYlay APULETcay.
Alackay ethay ayday, e’sshay eadday, e’sshay eadday, e’sshay ead!day

APULETcay.
A!hay Etlay emay eesay erhay. Outay alas!ay E’sshay oldcay,
Erhay oodblay isay ettledsay anday erhay ointsjay areay iffstay.
Ifelay anday esethay ipslay avehay onglay eenbay eparatedsay.
Eathday ieslay onay erhay ikelay anay untimelyay ostfray
Uponay ethay eetestsway owerflay ofay allay ethay ieldfay.

URSEnay.
Oay amentablelay ay!day

ADYlay APULETcay.
Oay ofulway ime!tay

APULETcay.
Eathday, atthay athhay a’entay erhay encehay otay akemay emay ailway,
Iestay upay myay onguetay anday illway otnay etlay emay eakspay.

Enteray Iarfray Awrencelay anday Arispay ithway Usiciansmay.

IARfRay AWRENCElay.
Omecay, isay ethay idebray eadyray otay ogay otay urch?chay

APULETcay.
Eadyray otay ogay, utbay evernay otay eturnray.
Oay onsay, ethay ightnay eforebay thyay eddingway ayday
Athhay eathday ainlay ithway thyay idebray. Erethay eshay ieslay,
Owerflay asay eshay asway, efloweredday byay imhay.
Eathday isay myay on-in-lawsay, eathday isay myay eir;hay
Myay aughterday ehay athhay eddedway. Iay illway ieday
Anday eavelay imhay all;ay ifelay, ivinglay, allay isay eath’sday.

ARISpay.
Avehay Iay oughtthay onglay otay eesay isthay orning’smay acefay,
Anday othday itay ivegay emay uchsay aay ightsay asay is?thay

ADYlay APULETcay.
Accurs’day, unhappyay, etchedwray, atefulhay ayday.
Ostmay iserablemay ourhay atthay e’eray imetay awsay
Inay astinglay abourlay ofay ishay ilgrimagepay.
Utbay oneay, oorpay oneay, oneay oorpay anday ovinglay ildchay,
Utbay oneay ingthay otay ejoiceray anday olacesay inay,
Anday uelcray eathday athhay atch’dcay itay omfray myay ightsay.

URSEnay.
Oay oe!way Oay oefulway, oefulway, oefulway ayday.
Ostmay amentablelay ayday, ostmay oefulway ayday

Eacepay, ohay, orfay ameshay. Onfusion’scay urecay iveslay otnay
Inay esethay onfusionscay. Eavenhay anday ourselfyay
Adhay artpay inay isthay airfay aidmay, ownay eavenhay athhay allay,
Anday allay ethay etterbay isay itay orfay ethay aidmay.
Ouryay artpay inay erhay ouyay ouldcay otnay eepkay omfray eathday,

Anday eepway eyay ownay, eeingsay eshay isay advanc’day
Aboveay ethay oudsclay, asay ighhay asay eavenhay itself?ay

ACTay Vay

ENEsCay Iay. Antuamay. Aay Eetstray.


Enteray Omeoray.

OMEOray.
Ifay Iay aymay usttray ethay atteringflay eyeay ofay eepslay,
Myay eamsdray esagepray omesay oyfuljay ewsnay atay andhay.
Myay osom’sbay ordlay itssay ightlylay inay ishay one;thray
Anday allay isthay ayday anay unaccustom’day iritspay
Iftslay emay aboveay ethay oundgray ithway eerfulchay oughtsthay.
Iay eamtdray myay adylay amecay anday oundfay emay ead—day,
Angestray eamdray, atthay ivesgay aay eadday anmay eavelay otay ink!—thay
Anday eath’dbray uchsay ifelay ithway isseskay inay myay ipslay,
Atthay Iay eviv’dray, anday asway anay emperoray.
Ahay emay, owhay eetsway isay ovelay itselfay ossess’dpay,
Enwhay utbay ove’slay adowsshay areay osay ichray inay oyjay.

Ellway, Ulietjay, Iay illway ielay ithway eethay onighttay.
Et’slay eesay orfay eansmay. Oay ischiefmay outhay artay iftsway
Otay enteray inay ethay oughtsthay ofay esperateday enmay.
Iay oday ememberray anay apothecary—ay,
Anday ereaboutshay ehay ells—whichdway, atelay Iay otednay
Inay atter’dtay eedsway, ithway overwhelmingay owsbray,
Ullingcay ofay implessay, eagremay ereway ishay ookslay,
Arpshay iserymay adhay ornway imhay otay ethay ones;bay
Anday inay ishay eedynay opshay aay ortoisetay unghay,
Anay alligatoray uff’dstay, anday otheray insskay
Ofay ill-shapeday ishes;fay anday aboutay ishay elvesshay
Aay eggarlybay accountay ofay emptyay oxesbay,
Eengray earthenay otspay, addersblay, anday ustymay eedssay,
Emnantsray ofay ackthreadpay, anday olday akescay ofay osesray
Ereway inlythay atter’dscay, otay akemay upay aay owshay.
Otingnay isthay enurypay, otay elfmysay Iay aidsay,
Anday ifay aay anmay idday eednay aay oisonpay ownay,
Osewhay alesay isay esentpray eathday inay Antuamay,
Erehay iveslay aay aitiffcay etchwray ouldway ellsay itay imhay.

Advances_][_ay.

ies_][_Day.

Otay inkthay itay asway o?say Oay, ivegay emay thyay andhay,
Oneay itwray ithway emay inay oursay isfortune’smay ookbay.
I’llay urybay eethay inay aay iumphanttray avegray.
Aay ave?gray Oay onay, aay anternlay, aught’redslay outhyay,
Orfay erehay ieslay Ulietjay, anday erhay eautybay akesmay
Isthay aultvay aay eastingfay esencepray ullfay ofay ightlay.
Eathday, ielay outhay erethay, byay aay eadday anmay interr’day.

aying[_Lay Arispay inay ethay onument_]may.

Owhay oftay enwhay enmay areay atay ethay ointpay ofay eathday
Avehay eythay eenbay erry!may Ichwhay eirthay eeperskay allcay
Aay ightninglay eforebay eathday. Oay, owhay aymay Iay
Allcay isthay aay ightning?lay Oay myay ovelay, myay ifeway,
Eathday atthay athhay uck’dsay ethay oneyhay ofay thyay eathbray,
Athhay adhay onay owerpay etyay uponay thyay eautybay.
Outhay artay otnay onquer’dcay. Eauty’sbay ensignay etyay
Isay imsoncray inay thyay ipslay anday inay thyay eekschay,
Anday eath’sday alepay agflay isay otnay advanceday erethay.
Alttybay, iestlay outhay erethay inay thyay oodyblay eet?shay
Oay, atwhay oremay avourfay ancay Iay oday otay eethay
Anthay ithway atthay andhay atthay utcay thyay outhyay inay aintway
Otay undersay ishay atthay asway inethay enemy?ay
Orgivefay emay, ousincay. Ahay, earday Ulietjay,
Whyay artay outhay etyay osay air?fay Allshay Iay elievebay
Atthay unsubstantialay eathday isay amorous;ay
Anday atthay ethay eanlay abhorreday onstermay eepskay
Eethay erehay inay arkday otay ebay ishay aramour?pay
Orfay earfay ofay atthay Iay illstay illway aystay ithway eethay,
Anday evernay omfray isthay alacepay ofay imday ightnay
Epartday againay. Erehay, erehay illway Iay emainray
Ithway ormsway atthay areay thyay ambermaidschay. Oay, erehay
Illway Iay etsay upay myay everlastingay est;ray
Anday akeshay ethay okeyay ofay inauspiciousay arsstay
Omfray isthay orld-weariedway eshflay. Eyesay, ooklay ouryay astlay.
Armsay, aketay ouryay astlay embrace!ay Anday, ipslay, Oay ouyay
Ethay oorsday ofay eathbray, ealsay ithway aay ighteousray isskay
Aay atelessday argainbay otay engrossingay eathday.
Omecay, itterbay onductcay, omecay, unsavouryay uidegay.
Outhay esperateday ilotpay, ownay atay onceay unray onay
Ethay ashingday ocksray thyay ea-sicksay earyway arkbay.
Ere’shay otay myay ove!lay inks_][_Dray. Oay uetray apothecary!ay
Thyay ugsdray areay uickqay. Usthay ithway aay isskay Iay ieday.
